# faucet-stream Matrix Machinery — Contract Map

Everything below is against repo root `/Users/c-sikawats/Documents/GitHub/Personal/faucet-stream`. The four focus files are large (config.rs 2150 lines, expand.rs 3033, executor.rs 3873); the structures and control flow you need are all in the first sections I quote below.

## 1. Verbatim struct definitions

### `MatrixRow` — `cli/src/config.rs:510-583`

```rust
/// One row of the `matrix:` block.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, JsonSchema)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct MatrixRow {
    /// Row identifier. ... Anonymous rows get a synthetic `row-N` id.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub id: Option&lt;String&gt;,

    /// If set, this row runs once per record produced by the named parent row.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub parent: Option&lt;String&gt;,

    /// Row ids this row waits for. ... Pure completion-ordering — unlike
    /// `parent:`, no records are consumed and no per-record fan-out occurs.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub depends_on: Vec&lt;String&gt;,

    /// Dotted field path inside each parent record that uniquely identifies
    /// the record. Used as the state-key suffix. Default: `id`.
    #[serde(default = "default_parent_key")]
    pub parent_key: String,

    /// Partial override of `pipeline.source` (deep-merged).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub source: Option&lt;PartialConnector&gt;,

    /// Partial override of `pipeline.sink` (deep-merged).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sink: Option&lt;PartialConnector&gt;,

    /// Row-level transforms. Appended after `T_pipeline` and `T_source` ...
    #[serde(default)]
    pub transforms: Option&lt;Vec&lt;TransformSpec&gt;&gt;,

    /// When `false`, drops upstream `T_pipeline` and `T_source` transforms
    /// for this row. Default `true`.
    #[serde(default = "default_true")]
    pub inherit_transforms: bool,

    /// If `Some`, replaces `pipeline.state` wholesale.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub state: Option&lt;StateStoreSpec&gt;,

    /// Matrix-row override semantics:
    /// - field absent  → `None`     — inherit from `pipeline.dlq`
    /// - `dlq: null`   → `Some(None)` — disable DLQ for this row
    /// - `dlq: { ... }` → `Some(Some(spec))` — replace base DLQ wholesale
    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = "deserialize_dlq_override")]
    pub dlq: Option&lt;Option&lt;DlqSpec&gt;&gt;,

    /// Per-row delivery override. `None` inherits the top-level `delivery`.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub delivery: Option&lt;faucet_core::DeliveryMode&gt;,

    /// Free-form classification tags for this row (#376). ...
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub tags: Vec&lt;String&gt;,

    /// Split this row into N independent invocations over a chunked range (#479) ...
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub partition: Option&lt;crate::partition::PartitionSpec&gt;,
}
```

Notes for your feature:
- `parent_key` default is `"id"` (`default_parent_key`, config.rs:1048).
- The row's `source`/`sink` are `Option&lt;PartialConnector&gt;` (config.rs:460-478), which carries `r#ref` (template name), `kind` (`type` override), `config` (deep-merge), and `status`. There is no `matrix` field on `MatrixRow` itself — the cross-product you want does not exist yet; a row is a single point, and fan-out today is only per-parent-record or per-partition-chunk.
- `PipelineConfig.matrix` is `Vec&lt;MatrixRow&gt;` at **config.rs:80** (`#[serde(default)]`; empty = one anonymous row). Topology mode (`pipeline.nodes`) is mutually exclusive with a non-empty `matrix` (config.rs:238-248).

### `ExpandedNode` — `cli/src/expand.rs:40-114`

```rust
/// One fully-merged matrix row, ready for the executor.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ExpandedNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub row_index: usize,
    pub role: NodeRole,
    pub source: ConnectorSpec,
    pub sink: ConnectorSpec,
    pub transforms: Vec&lt;TransformSpec&gt;,
    pub state: Option&lt;StateStoreSpec&gt;,
    /// Resolved DLQ spec for this row, or `None` if no DLQ applies.
    pub dlq: Option&lt;crate::config::DlqSpec&gt;,
    #[cfg(feature = "quality")]
    pub quality: Option&lt;faucet_core::QualitySpec&gt;,
    #[cfg(feature = "contract")]
    pub contract: Option&lt;faucet_core::ContractSpec&gt;,
    #[cfg(feature = "masking")]
    pub masking: Option&lt;faucet_core::MaskingSpec&gt;,
    /// The sink template name this node resolved (`sink.ref`, or `"default"`) ...
    pub sink_ref: String,
    /// Compiled schema-drift policy spec (pipeline-level; same for every node).
    pub schema: Option&lt;faucet_core::SchemaDriftSpec&gt;,
    /// Delivery guarantee for this row. ...
    pub delivery: faucet_core::DeliveryMode,
    /// The **derived** end-to-end guarantee this row's source × sink × config
    /// actually provides (issue #292) ...
    pub delivery_guarantee: faucet_core::DeliveryGuarantee,
    /// Row ids this node waits for (deduplicated, declaration order). ...
    pub depends_on: Vec&lt;String&gt;,
    /// Resolved readiness status for this row's source (#371) ...
    pub status: crate::config::SourceStatus,
    /// Effective classification tags (#376) = source-template `tags` ∪ row `tags` ...
    pub tags: Vec&lt;String&gt;,
    /// Every `${id.path}` placeholder that survived load-time interpolation. ...
    pub deferred_refs: Vec&lt;DeferredRef&gt;,
    /// A pre-built source that replaces the registry-built one for this node.
    /// Set only by `faucet dlq replay` (#281) ...
    pub source_override: Option&lt;crate::dlq_replay::reader::SourceOverride&gt;,
    /// Scoped-cleanup claim (#478) ...
    pub cleanup_scope: Option&lt;std::collections::BTreeMap&lt;String, serde_json::Value&gt;&gt;,
}
```

Supporting enums/structs (expand.rs:116-132):

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum NodeRole {
    Root,
    Child { parent_id: String, parent_key: String },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct DeferredRef {
    pub referenced_id: String,   // the `id` in ${id.path}
    pub dotted_path: String,     // the `path` in ${id.path}
    pub token: String,           // the literal `${id.path}` token text
}
```

`source_override` is the escape hatch already used to inject a pre-built source (DLQ replay); your discovery feature could reuse the same idea if you need to substitute a runtime-built source.

## 2. How a `parent:` child gets its N invocations at runtime

The whole runtime lives in `run_expanded()` — **executor.rs:216-568** — which runs **level-by-level** (roots = level 0), not a recursive walk.

Data structures:
- `CapturedRecords = Arc&lt;Mutex&lt;HashMap&lt;String, Vec&lt;Arc&lt;Value&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;` — **executor.rs:46**. Keyed by node id; holds each parent's emitted records as `Arc&lt;Value&gt;` (pointer-clone hand-off, #160).
- A parent's records are captured by wrapping its sink in a `CapturingSink` (constructed in `run_one_invocation`, executor.rs:1088-1096) and, on success, appended to the map in `run_unit` (**executor.rs:606-617**): `.extend(records.into_iter().map(Arc::new))`. Capture only happens when `capture.is_some()`, i.e. the node has children (see `build_projections`).
- Only the referenced fields are kept: `build_projections()` (**executor.rs:778-817**) computes a `Projection::Full` or `Projection::Paths(...)` per parent from each child's `parent_key` plus its `deferred_refs`. Applied via `project_record` (executor.rs:759).

Per-level fan-out (**executor.rs:334-424**):
1. `ready` = every remaining node whose parent and all `depends_on` are terminal (executor.rs:290-308).
2. `level_records` drains the consumed parents' records out of the shared map (executor.rs:334-347).
3. For a `NodeRole::Child`, it fetches `parent_records = level_records.get(parent_id)` and loops over **each record**, building one `Unit` per record (**executor.rs:387-423**). Empty parent output → child skipped (executor.rs:392-398).

The per-record context map passed to interpolation is built in **`run_one_invocation`, executor.rs:1037-1044**:

```rust
if let (Some(record), NodeRole::Child { parent_id, .. }) = (parent_record, &amp;node.role) {
    let ctx: HashMap&lt;String, Value&gt; = HashMap::from([(parent_id.clone(), record.clone())]);
    resolve_inplace(&amp;mut source_cfg, &amp;ctx)?;
    resolve_inplace(&amp;mut sink_cfg, &amp;ctx)?;
    if let Some(scope) = cleanup_scope.as_mut() {
        resolve_inplace(scope, &amp;ctx)?;
    }
}
```

So the ctx map shape is `{ parent_id =&gt; full_parent_record_json }` — a single entry keyed by the parent's row id. `resolve_inplace` (a local helper in executor.rs that walks strings calling `interpolate_record`) is what expands `${parent_id.dotted.path}` per record. Note `${now.*}` is resolved *before* this, on every invocation, via `resolve_now_inplace` (executor.rs:1015-1016).

Concurrency model (executor.rs:444-461): all units of a level are spawned into a `tokio::task::JoinSet`; each task first `sem.acquire().await` on a shared `Arc&lt;Semaphore&gt;` (executor.rs:228, size `max_concurrent` = config or `default_concurrency()` clamped 1..=8, executor.rs:207-212). Roots and children share the one budget.

## 3. Per-invocation state key + `build_state_key`

**executor.rs:641-651**:

```rust
/// Produce `{pipeline_name}::{row_id}` or `{pipeline_name}::{row_id}::{key}`.
pub(crate) fn build_state_key(
    pipeline_name: &amp;str,
    row_id: &amp;str,
    parent_key: Option&lt;&amp;str&gt;,
) -&gt; String {
    match parent_key {
        None =&gt; format!("{pipeline_name}::{row_id}"),
        Some(k) =&gt; format!("{pipeline_name}::{row_id}::{k}"),
    }
}
```

Callers:
- Root: `build_state_key(&amp;opts.pipeline_name, &amp;node.id, None)` — **executor.rs:378**.
- Child: **executor.rs:402-422**. For each parent record it computes `pk_value = resolve_parent_key(record, parent_key)` (executor.rs:671-681, dotted-path walk) then `pk_string = value_to_string_brief(...)` (falls back to `"(missing)"`), then `build_state_key(&amp;opts.pipeline_name, &amp;node.id, Some(&amp;pk_string))`. So the child key is `{name}::{row}::{parent_record_key}`.
- Collision guard: siblings sharing a parent are checked into a `seen_keys` set; a repeat is `CliError::DuplicateStateKey` (executor.rs:411-416).
- `validate_unit_state_key` (executor.rs:657-668) validates the key up front only when `uses_state`.

There is a further shard suffix in `run_one_invocation`: `effective_state_key = format!("{state_key}::{}", shard.id)` (executor.rs:1208-1211), and partitioned rows carry a `::partition::{chunk}` suffix baked into the node id at expand time (expand.rs:1033).

`StateKeyOverride` — **executor.rs:1212-1219** — wraps the built source so the executor's per-row key overrides the source's natural (kind-shared) key:

```rust
let source: Box&lt;dyn Source&gt; = if state.is_some() &amp;&amp; source.state_key().is_some() {
    Box::new(StateKeyOverride { inner: source, key: effective_state_key })
} else {
    source
};
```

## 4. RESERVED_IDS and reserved interpolation-id prefixes

`RESERVED_IDS` — **expand.rs:27-37**:

```rust
pub const RESERVED_IDS: &amp;[&amp;str] = &amp;[
    "env", "file", "secret", "matrix", "pipeline",
    "now", "backfill", "param", "partition",
];
```

Enforced on row ids at **expand.rs:307-309** (`CliError::ReservedRowId`). Duplicate ids → `DuplicateRowId` (expand.rs:310-313).

Reserved runtime/deferred interpolation ids (not parent-record refs) are handled specially in three places:
- **`check_refs`** (expand.rs:1122-1159) is where an unknown `${id.path}` is rejected. It uses `iter_directives` + `classify_directive` (interpolate.rs). For a `Directive::Deferred { id, .. }`: `param` → hard error (never-bound, expand.rs:1135-1144); otherwise if `id != "now" &amp;&amp; id != "backfill" &amp;&amp; id != "partition" &amp;&amp; !id_set.contains(id)` → `CliError::UnknownInterpolationId` (expand.rs:1145-1155). `env`/`file`/`secret` never reach here because with a colon they classify as `LoadTime` and are already resolved.
- `check_refs` is called over every row's source/sink config and the pipeline-level source/sink/sources/sinks (expand.rs:370-394).
- `collect_deferred` (expand.rs:1220-1240) skips `now`/`backfill`/`partition` so they aren't treated as parent-record deps; everything else becomes a `DeferredRef`.
- `reject_runtime_tokens` (expand.rs:1168-1181) rejects *any* deferred token in transform/state/dlq configs — runtime `${...}` tokens are only legal in source/sink configs.

The classification rule itself is `classify_directive` — **interpolate.rs:250-258**: colon → `LoadTime { prefix, body }`; else split on first `.` → `Deferred { id, path }`.

## 5. Where a runtime "run a source, project a field, dedup" step hooks in

Two existing precedents build+run a source with **no sink** — both are your best hook models:

1. **`faucet discover`** — `cli/src/commands/discover.rs` (this is the closest existing thing to your feature, issue #211). `run()` at discover.rs:19-110 does: `build_source(&amp;spec.kind, spec.config.clone(), &amp;auth, None)` (discover.rs:39-40), checks `source.supports_discover()`, then `source.discover().await` to enumerate datasets, filters them (`filter_datasets`), and **generates a config with one matrix row per dataset** (`render_discovered_config`, discover.rs:80). That is fan-out over discovered values, but resolved *ahead of run* into static rows, not at runtime.

2. **`faucet preview`** — `cli/src/commands/preview.rs:54-77`: `build_source(...)` then `let records = source.fetch_all().await?;`, applies transform stages, takes `args.limit`, prints. No sink.

3. **`reconcile::run`** — `cli/src/reconcile.rs:123-139`: the canonical minimal "build a source, drain it, extract a field" pattern:

```rust
let source = crate::registry::build_source(&amp;spec.count.kind, spec.count.config.clone(), auth, None).await?;
let records = source.fetch_all().await?;
let authoritative = extract_count(&amp;records, spec.count.count_field.as_deref())?;
```

The underlying primitive is the `Source` trait — `crates/core/src/traits.rs:12-48`:
- `fetch_with_context(&amp;self, context: &amp;HashMap&lt;String, Value&gt;)` (traits.rs:20) — the primary method; **this is exactly the context-map contract your discovery step could feed**, same shape as the executor's per-record `ctx`.
- `fetch_all(&amp;self)` (traits.rs:26) — convenience with empty context.
- `stream_pages(...)` (traits.rs:73) for paged draining.

Field projection uses `resolve_dotted(root, path)` (interpolate.rs:328-344), and the executor's `resolve_parent_key` / `walk_value` (executor.rs:671-681, 720-730) are the dotted-path readers you'd mirror. There is **no existing dedup helper** — you would add that.

Assessment: the executor does *not* currently have a runtime "enumerate a value-set from a source then fan out rows over it" step. The per-parent-record fan-out (executor.rs:387-423) is the structural analog: it takes a `Vec&lt;Arc&lt;Value&gt;&gt;` and emits one Unit per element. A discovery-driven matrix would most naturally either (a) resolve to static rows before the run like `discover` does, or (b) introduce a new pre-level in `run_expanded` that runs discovery sources (via `build_source` + `fetch_all`), projects+dedups values, and synthesizes `Unit`s the way the child branch does — feeding the cross-product into the same `ctx` map / `build_state_key` machinery. The Unit-per-element loop and `CapturedRecords` are your templates; a cross-product would multiply several such value-sets before building units.

## 6. How `depends_on` completion-ordering interacts with `parent:`

They are **orthogonal and combinable** — a node can have both.

- **Expand-time**: `parent` and `depends_on` are validated independently. Parents: single-parent-chain cycle check `detect_cycle` (expand.rs:1045-1065). `depends_on`: unknown-dep / self-dep checks + per-row dedup preserving order (expand.rs:343-364). Then the **combined** graph is checked with **Kahn's algorithm** in `detect_combined_cycle` (**expand.rs:1073-1117**): for each row, `prereqs = parent (if any) ∪ depends_on`, computes in-degrees, topo-sorts; any unprocessed node → `CliError::DependencyCycle`. This is why a cycle routed *through* a `depends_on` edge can't deadlock the executor.
- The BFS `order` that `expand()` emits (expand.rs:410-418) is built from the **parent** graph only (roots first, then children breadth-first) — `depends_on` does **not** affect emission order; it is enforced purely at runtime.
- **Runtime readiness** (`run_expanded`, **executor.rs:290-308**): a node is `ready` only when BOTH hold:
  - parent is terminal: `Root =&gt; true`, else `completed.contains(parent_id) || skipped_subtrees.contains(parent_id)`;
  - AND `node.depends_on.iter().all(|d| completed.contains(d) || skipped_subtrees.contains(d))`.
- **Difference in semantics**: `parent:` hands off records (the child fans out once per parent record, consuming `level_records`); `depends_on:` is pure completion-ordering — no records consumed, no fan-out (see the module doc, executor.rs:13-17, and `MatrixRow.depends_on` doc, config.rs:522-527).
- **Skip cascade**: if a parent failed/was skipped, the child subtree is skipped (executor.rs:352-358); if any `depends_on` prerequisite failed/was skipped, the node is skipped too (executor.rs:363-374). Both cascade into `skipped_subtrees`. Readiness only asks "is there anything left to wait for"; whether a terminal prereq *skips* the node is decided in the unit loop.

A node with both `parent: p` and `depends_on: [d]` therefore fans out per record of `p` **and** waits for `d` to fully complete first; readiness requires both terminal, and a failure of either skips it.

## Meltano tap mechanism analysis (DrivetrainAi)

All six are custom DrivetrainAi taps built on the Singer SDK, run under Meltano, loading to `target-bigquery` (z3z1ma). Stream selection is via a `streams_to_discover`/`streams_to_sync` CSV in every tap. Files cited are under `g3/&lt;name&gt;/tap_&lt;name&gt;/` unless noted.

---

### RILLET (ERP / accounting close software)
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON to `https://api.rillet.com`, Singer SDK `RESTStream`. Report endpoints pin header `X-Rillet-API-Version: 3`. (`streams.py`, `tap.py`)
- **Auth:** Bearer static token (`auth_token`) via `BearerTokenAuthenticator`. (`streams.py:221-224`)
- **Pagination:** cursor-in-body — `$.pagination.next_cursor` → `cursor` query param, `limit=100`. Report streams are single-response (`next_page_token_jsonpath = None`). (`streams.py:205,236-242`)
- **Streams (static classes, filtered by `streams_to_sync`):** two families.
  - *Collection streams* (append-ish, cursor-paged): account, bank_account, bill, book_period, credit_memo, customer, field, invoice, journal_entry, organization, product, subsidiary, vendor, journal_entry_report.
  - *Report streams* — the parity-heavy part, driven by the SDK `partitions` property (each partition = one API call), NOT parent-child streams:
    - **balance_sheet_report** (`/reports/balance-sheet`): partitions = **month-end dates** from `start_date` to today (`_iter_month_ends`); each call `breakdown_by=SUBSIDIARY`. (`streams.py:724-740`)
    - **income_statement_report / cash_flow_statement_report** (`_PeriodReportStream`): partitions = **(month-window) × (breakdown_spec)**. Windows are first→last-of-month `(from_date,to_date)` via `_iter_month_ranges`; breakdown_specs = one `SUBSIDIARY` call **plus one `CUSTOM_FIELD` call per custom-field id**, where field ids are fetched on-the-fly by paginating `/fields` (`_fetch_collection_ids`, cached). CUSTOM_FIELD requires an extra `custom_field_id` query param. (`streams.py:755-866`)
    - **trial_balance_report** (`/reports/trial-balance`): partitions = **month-window × subsidiary_id**. Subsidiary id list = **union of `/subsidiaries` and the SUBSIDIARY breakdowns pulled from a live `/reports/balance-sheet` call** (`_fetch_subsidiary_breakdown_ids`) — the breakdown source adds intercompany *elimination entities* that `/subsidiaries` omits (ENG-81840; without them the summed TB doesn't reconcile to the BS). Falls back to a single consolidated call (`subsidiary_id=None`) if no subsidiaries. (`streams.py:869-958`)
- **Incremental:** none — no `replication_key`. Full re-pull every run bounded only by `start_date` month iteration; `target-bigquery` is `overwrite: true`.
- **Record shaping (heavy):** `flatten_dict` on every row; `unnest_list_config` explodes nested line-item arrays into child rows (bill/credit_memo/invoice/journal_entry `items`, field `values`) with composite PKs like `[id, item_id]`. Report parsers **flatten the entire report tree** by hand: recursive `_walk_balance_sheet_tree` / `_walk_sections` emit one leaf row per (account × subsidiary breakdown), denormalizing section/group/subgroup totals onto each row, dropping consolidated row-totals, and synthesizing "rolled-up" Cash lines from subgroup totals where Rillet hides bank accounts (`_is_rolled_up_leaf`). `item_fields` JSON-stringified. Custom 429 handling (constant 65s backoff, no Retry-After header).
- **Parity risk: VERY HIGH.** The report fan-out (month windows × subsidiaries × custom-fields), the on-the-fly id discovery via extra un-paginated helper calls, the elimination-entity union, and the hand-rolled hierarchical report-tree flattening with reconciliation-specific rules are all bespoke tap logic with no generic-connector equivalent.

---

### STRIPE (payments)
- **Mechanism:** **Vendor SDK** — the official `stripe` Python SDK (`sdk_object.list()`, `stripe.Charge`/`Invoice`/etc. in `constants.SDK_OBJECTS`) for entity streams. Plus a **separate Reporting-API path** (`reportbase.py`) using raw `requests` against `https://api.stripe.com/v1/reporting`. (`base.py:206-213`, `reportbase.py`)
- **Auth:** API key. SDK path sets `stripe.api_key`; report path uses HTTP Basic (`api_key` as user). Org keys (`sk_org_live_*`) add a `Stripe-Context` header (+ pinned `Stripe-Version: 2023-10-16`). (`base.py:228-231`, `reportbase.py:550-576`)
- **Pagination:** SDK path — manual cursor loop via `starting_after=last.id` + `has_more` (re-issues full params each page so the org context header survives). Report path — issue report run → poll download URL → stream CSV. (`base.py:363-380`, `reportbase.py:642-828`)
- **Streams (static):** ~23 entity streams (accounts, charges, invoices, customers, subscriptions, payouts, refunds, disputes, credit_notes, balance_transactions, etc.) + **subscription_history** + one **report stream** `card_payments_fees_transaction_level_1` (report_type `card_payments_fees.transaction_level.1`).
- **Incremental:** two modes. `is_immutable=True` streams replicate on `created` directly. Mutable streams (checkout_sessions, discounts, subscriptions, subscription_history) replicate via the **Stripe Events API** — `sdk_object = stripe.Event` filtered by `EVENT_TYPE_FILTERS` type globs, unwrapping `data.object`. Bookmark key `created` (or `date`). Time window is **chunked into half-month slices** and fetched with a `ThreadPoolExecutor` (`max_workers`, default 9); disputes use a 90-day lookback + 90-day chunks. Event streams **dedupe by object id** (latest-created wins) to avoid MERGE conflicts. (`base.py:185-494`)
- **Record shaping:** `flatten_keys` (dict → `key_subkey`, nested → JSON string), `string_keys` (JSON-stringify), event `record_path` unwrap, extra `created`/`event_id` keys. Report path parses CSV rows to dicts. (`base.py:299-345`)
- **Parity risk: HIGH.** Reliance on the official Stripe SDK, the Events-API-as-incremental model with dedup, threaded month-chunk fetching, and the async report-run/poll/CSV-download flow.

---

### HUBSPOT (CRM)
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON, HubSpot CRM v3. Core object reads via **POST `/crm/v3/objects/{type}/search`** (search endpoint), plus `/crm/v3/properties/{type}` (schema), `/crm/v3/schemas` (custom-object resolution), `/crm/v3/objects/{type}/batch/read`, `/crm/v3/lists/{id}/memberships`. Raw `requests` via `utils/req.py` (SDK `_request` is monkey-patched to route POST search through it). (`streams/object.py:278-280,720-735`)
- **Auth:** **OAuth2 refresh-token** flow — exchanges `client_id`/`client_secret`/`refresh_token` at `https://api.hubspot.com/oauth/v1/token`, caches access token with 10-min buffer. (`auth.py`)
- **Pagination:** cursor-in-body — search `paging.next.after` → payload `after`; **`limit:200`, sorted by id ASC**, with an id-filter workaround (`hs_object_id GT last_id`) to break past the search API's hard 10k-result cap. (`streams/object.py:362-436,783-791`)
- **Streams (heavily discovered/dynamic):** `tap.py::discover_streams` runs a multi-pass matcher over the requested names against object/association/user/history/analytics registries, then dynamically synthesizes classes for: **list-membership streams** (`list_&lt;id&gt;`), **custom objects** (resolved by name against `/crm/v3/schemas`, e.g. `agreement`→`p21230183_agreement`), **custom-object property-history** (`&lt;obj&gt;_property_history`), and **custom associations** (`&lt;from&gt;_to_&lt;to&gt;`), building `type(...)` subclasses at runtime and wiring `parent_stream_type`.
- **Parent→child fan-out:** object streams accumulate ids across `child_batch_pages` (default 5) then dispatch child association/history streams **concurrently** (`ThreadPoolExecutor`, `sibling_child_workers=3`) via `/batch/read` (sub-batched 200, 5 workers). Deals fan out to deal_company/deal_contact/deal_property_history/merged deals. (`streams/object.py:197-276,523-551`)
- **Incremental:** search filter `hs_lastmodifieddate GTE` bookmark; also pulls **archived** records separately (GET list, or ID-enumerate + batch/read when &gt;250 properties). (`streams/object.py:365-436,553-604`)
- **Record shaping:** the `properties` bag is popped and flattened to `property_&lt;name&gt;` columns (raw bag dropped to halve BQ size); string-"true"/"false"→bool coercion; epoch-ms→ISO date coercion for date-typed props; `schema_override` renames. **Notably the tap issues BigQuery DELETE-before-emit for association children** (tap reaches into the warehouse directly). (`streams/object.py:470-521`; `utils/bigquery.py`)
- **Parity risk: VERY HIGH.** POST-search cursor-in-body + 10k id-filter workaround, runtime class synthesis for custom objects/associations/history, concurrent parent→child batch-read fan-out, archived-record second pass, and the direct-to-BigQuery DELETE side-channel all far exceed a declarative REST connector.

---

### RAMP (corporate cards / spend)
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON to `https://api.ramp.com/developer/v1`, Singer SDK `RESTStream` (`meltano_utils.DriveRestStream` indirectly). (`base.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Auth:** **OAuth2 client_credentials** — POST to `/token` with HTTP Basic (`client_id`/`client_secret`) + space-separated `scopes` (large default scope list in `tap.py`); token cached with 5-min refresh buffer, shared across all streams. (`auth.py`)
- **Pagination:** HATEOAS — full next-page URL under `page.next` (`BaseHATEOASPaginator`), `page_size=100`; single-object streams use `SinglePagePaginator`. (`base.py:8-14,44-54`)
- **Streams (static, ~22):** business, business_balance, entities, bills, bill_drafts, cards, departments, limits, locations, merchants, purchase_orders, receipts, reimbursements, spend_programs, transactions, users, vendors, statements, transfers, repayments, cashbacks, accounts, balance_history. `business`/`business_balance` are single-object. (`streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** none (no `replication_key`); server-side date filters only on first-page params: transactions/reimbursements `from_date`, bills `from_created_at` (from config `start_date`). BQ loader `overwrite: true`. (`streams.py:91-95,200-204,236-240`)
- **Record shaping:** per-stream `convert_to_string` JSON-stringifies nested fields (`stringify_complex_fields`) for flat BQ columns. (`base.py:56-57`)
- **Parity risk: LOW.** Straightforward paginated REST + OAuth2 client-credentials + per-stream date filter; maps cleanly to a generic REST source.

---

### BREX (corporate cards / spend)
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON to `https://platform.brexapis.com`, Singer SDK `RESTStream` (`DriveRestStream`). (`base.py`)
- **Auth:** **static API key** as Bearer header (`APIKeyAuthenticator`, `Authorization: Bearer {api_key}`). No token exchange. (`base.py:38-46`)
- **Pagination:** cursor-in-body — `next_cursor` in response → `cursor` query param, `limit` up to 1000, records at `$.items[*]`. (`base.py:8-20,49-70`)
- **Streams (static, 6):** users (`/v2/users`), cards (`/v2/cards`), departments (`/v2/departments`), expenses (`/v1/expenses`), primary_card_account_transaction (`/v2/transactions/card/primary`), vendors (`/v1/vendors`). (`streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** none (no `replication_key`); full pull, BQ `overwrite: true`.
- **Record shaping:** `expand[]` query params to hydrate related objects (e.g. expenses expand location/department/merchant/user/budget/payment), `flatten_keys` (dict→`key_subkey`), `convert_to_string` (JSON-stringify). (`base.py:71-90`, `streams.py`)
- **Parity risk: LOW.** Clean cursor-paged REST with Bearer key; the only nuance is `expand[]` array query params.

---

### PLAID (bank-account aggregation)
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON, **POST-only** to `https://{sandbox|production}.plaid.com` (env-dependent host). Uses raw `requests` via `DriveRestStream` — **NOT** the Plaid vendor SDK, despite the "Plaid" keyword in pyproject. `Plaid-Version` header pinned (default `2020-09-14`). (`base.py:118-165`)
- **Auth:** credentials in the **request body** (`client_id` + `secret` + per-Item `access_token`), not headers. Access token is resolved by `item_id` at request time and deliberately kept out of partition context/STATE/logs. (`base.py:190-233`)
- **Pagination:** cursor. transactions use `/transactions/sync`'s `has_more`+`next_cursor` (`PlaidSyncPaginator`); accounts are single-shot. (`streams.py:64-92`)
- **Streams (static, 2):** accounts (`POST /accounts/get`), transactions (`POST /transactions/sync`).
- **Plaid Item / access-token model:** one **partition per linked bank (Item)**. Config carries a single scalar `access_token`+`item_id` (a connection instance = one Item), wrapped into a one-element internal list; a deprecated `access_tokens` JSON-array fallback still supports multi-Item. STATE is partitioned by `item_id` (`state_partitioning_keys=["item_id"]`). (`base.py:60-165`, `streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** transactions use Plaid's **cursor sync** (not a timestamp key) — first run per Item = no cursor → full history; cursor saved to STATE **per page** (resumable mid-run); later runs send saved cursor for deltas. Emits `added`+`modified`; `removed` is logged but **not propagated** (upsert loader doesn't hard-delete — an explicit soft-delete gap). accounts are full-refresh (balances snapshot). `start_date` filter applied only on the first full run. (`streams.py:94-260`)
- **Record shaping:** flatten `balances`→`balances_*`, JSON-stringify nested txn fields (location, payment_meta, personal_finance_category, counterparties, category), stamp `item_id`/`institution_name`, composite PKs `[transaction_id,item_id]`/`[account_id,item_id]`. Fatal-vs-retriable Plaid error-code classification. (`base.py:8-20,254-296`, `streams.py`)
- **Parity risk: MEDIUM-HIGH.** POST-body auth, the per-Item partition + access-token-by-item_id resolution, the cursor-sync incremental model with per-page STATE, and the unhandled `removed`/soft-delete gap are all Plaid-specific behaviors a generic REST source won't cover.

---

**Cross-cutting notes:** every tap does record shaping to flatten nested JSON into BQ-friendly flat string columns (either the tap's own `flatten_dict`/`convert_to_string`/`flatten_keys` or Singer post_process). Rillet and HubSpot are the two extreme parity risks (bespoke report-tree fan-out; runtime-synthesized discovered streams + direct-BQ side effects). Ramp and Brex are the cleanest. Stripe's vendor-SDK + Events-API-incremental + async report path is a distinct mechanism class. Plaid's item/access-token model is its defining complexity. The `meltano_taps/` clone was left intact.</result>

## tap-rippling
- **Source system / category:** Rippling — HRIS / people data.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON. `url_base=https://api.rippling.com/platform/api`, single stream `employee` → `GET /employees/include_terminated`. Records at `$.[*]` (bare array). (`streams.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 refresh-token grant. Token endpoint `https://api.rippling.com/api/o/token/`, HTTP-Basic client_id/secret header + `grant_type=refresh_token`. Rippling **rotates the refresh_token every refresh**; tap writes the new one to `/tmp/refresh_token.txt`, and `post.py` persists it back to Drive. (`auth.py`)
- **Pagination:** Configurable. Default `page_size=0` → single unpaginated request. If &gt;0, offset-based (`limit`/`offset`, stops on short page). (`streams.py`)
- **Streams:** One static stream (`Employees`). No fan-out.
- **Incremental:** None — full-table each run (`fix_timestamp_keys` on created_at/updated_at only for formatting; no replication_key).
- **Record shaping:** `standardize_data` = rename_keys + `flatten_dict` (except `work_schedule`) + timestamp normalization; `send_all_roles=True`.
- **Parity risk:** MED — rotating refresh-token persistence loop (needs write-back side-effect); nested-dict flattening semantics must match.

## tap-deel
- **Source system / category:** Deel — global payroll / HRIS.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON. `url_base=https://api.letsdeel.com/rest/v2`. (`streams.py`)
- **Auth:** Static bearer token (`BearerTokenAuthenticator`, config `auth_token`). No OAuth/rotation. (`streams.py`)
- **Pagination:** Mixed per stream — `people`: single-page (`limit=200`); `legal_entities`: **cursor** JSONPath `$.page.cursor` (limit capped 100); `gp_legal_entity_reports`: single-page (unpaginated); `gp_gross_to_net`: **offset** paginator stopping at `page.total_rows`. (`streams.py`)
- **Streams:** `people` always synced; `legal_entities`, `gp_legal_entity_reports` (child of legal_entities, per-entity fan-out `{legal_entity_id}`), `gp_gross_to_net` (child of reports, per-report fan-out `{report_id}`). `additional_streams_to_sync` opt-in auto-pulls parents. Children set `state_partitioning_keys=[]`. (`tap.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** None (all `replication_key=None`).
- **Record shaping:** Heavy. `gp_gross_to_net` **unpivots** each report row's dynamic column map into tall records per `(report_id,row_index,column_key)`, copying identity fields (contract_id/worker_id/etc.), stringifying mixed-type cells, dropping the `employeeInfo` roster blob. `legal_entities` renames camelCase→snake. Child post_process injects parent ids.
- **Parity risk:** HIGH — parent→child fan-out (2 levels) + dynamic-column unpivot with computed primary key and identity denormalization.

## tap-greenhouse
- **Source system / category:** Greenhouse (Harvest v3) — ATS / recruiting.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON. `url_base=https://harvest.greenhouse.io/v3`, records `$[*]` (top-level array). (`base.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 **client_credentials**. Token endpoint `https://auth.greenhouse.io/token`, HTTP-Basic client_id/secret + `grant_type=client_credentials` → bearer. Note: only one active token per client_id; custom `_request` handles sibling-process token-revocation 401s with invalidate+backoff retry. (`base.py`)
- **Pagination:** **Link header** (`HeaderLinkPaginator`, `rel="next"`); follow-up pages must send only the cursor query (else 422). (`base.py`)
- **Streams:** 13 static streams selected via `streams_to_discover` (jobs, departments, applications, candidates, job_stages, offers, offices, reject_reasons, remote_users→`/users`, scheduled_interviews→`/interviews`, scorecards, openings, close_reasons). No fan-out. (`tap.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** Yes — per-stream `replication_key` (mostly `updated_at`, applications=`last_activity_at`) using v3 `gte|&lt;iso&gt;` filter operator. `close_reasons` full-table (no updated_at). (`base.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Record shaping:** `post_process` JSON-stringifies every nested dict/list for BigQuery.
- **Parity risk:** MED — client_credentials + single-active-token revocation recovery; `gte|` filter operator syntax.

## tap-lever
- **Source system / category:** Lever — ATS / recruiting.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON. `url_base=https://api.lever.co/v1`, records `$.data[*]`, next-page at `$.next`. (`client.py`)
- **Auth:** HTTP **Basic** — `api_key` as username, empty password (`BasicAuthenticator`). (`client.py`)
- **Pagination:** Offset/cursor via `next` token → `offset` param (`next_page_token_jsonpath=$.next`). Opportunities/requisitions add `limit=100`. (`client.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Streams:** 14 static, `streams_to_discover`-selected. `opportunities` is parent; 7 child streams (`application`, `offer`, `attachment`/`opportunity_resumes`→`/resumes`, `referral`, `panel`, `form`, `interviews`) fan out per `{opportunity_id}`. **Children synced in parallel via ThreadPoolExecutor (max 10 workers)** — custom `_sync_children` override. (`tap.py`, `client.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** `opportunities` INCREMENTAL on `updatedAt` (custom state, `updated_at_start` epoch param, custom `_increment_stream_state`); `requisitions` filters `created_at_start`. Others full-table. Children mark per-partition state `completed`. (`streams.py`)
- **Record shaping:** `denormalize_keys` (flatten a dict into `key_field` cols, e.g. `archived`, `file`), `convert_to_string` (JSON-stringify list/dict cols), `epoch_type_columns` epoch→datetime conversion. Per-stream configs.
- **Parity risk:** HIGH — parent→child per-opportunity fan-out run **concurrently with threads**, custom incremental state on epoch timestamps, per-partition completion markers.

## tap-bullhorn
- **Source system / category:** Bullhorn ATS — staffing/recruiting.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON over Bullhorn `search` endpoints (Lucene). Base URL is **dynamic** (`restUrl` returned by login); paths `/search/&lt;Entity&gt;` (Candidate, ClientContact, ClientCorporation, JobOrder, Placement, CorporateUser, JobSubmission, Note). Records at `data`. (`base.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Auth:** **CONFIRMED two-step session-login flow.** (1) OAuth2 refresh_token grant → `POST {auth_host}/oauth/token` (default `auth.bullhornstaffing.com`) yields access_token (rotates refresh_token → `/tmp/refresh_token.txt`, `post.py` persists). (2) `GET {login_host}/rest-services/login?version=*&amp;access_token=…&amp;ttl=` → `restUrl` + `BhRestToken`. Session cached with TTL (default 1440 min) + 300s buffer; `BhRestToken` stamped on every request URL; 401 → invalidate + re-login + retry. (`auth.py`, `base.py`)
- **Pagination:** Offset (`start`/`count`, `BullhornOffsetPaginator`) stopping at `total`. `count` default 20 (search max). (`base.py`)
- **Streams:** 8 static (`streams_to_discover` or all). No parent/child fan-out; explicit `fields` list per entity (not `*` when defined).
- **Incremental:** Yes — all search streams `replication_key=dateLastModified` (epoch-ms), Lucene range `query=dateLastModified:[&lt;low&gt; TO *]` + `sort`. `start_date` default 2023-01-01. (`base.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Record shaping:** Minimal — extract `data`, explicit field projection; 429 `Retry-After` honored.
- **Parity risk:** HIGH — the session-login flow (refresh→access_token→login→BhRestToken), dynamic per-session base URL, token-in-URL re-stamping on retry, and Lucene range-query incremental all need faithful replication.

## tap-navan
- **Source system / category:** Navan (formerly TripActions) — travel &amp; expense.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON. `url_base=https://api.navan.com`; `booking`→`/v1/bookings` (records `$.data[*]`), `transaction`→`/v1/expense/transactions` (records `$.content[*]`). (`base.py`, `streams.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 **client_credentials**. Token endpoint `https://api.navan.com/ta-auth/oauth/token`, form body client_id/secret → bearer; cached, refreshed 600s early. No rotation. (`auth.py`)
- **Pagination:** Two styles — `booking`: **page-number** (`NavanPagePaginator`, 0-indexed via `page.currentPage`/`totalPages`, `size=100`); `transaction`: **cursor** (`NavanCursorPaginator`, `has_next`/`next_cursor`). (`base.py`, `streams.py`)
- **Streams:** 2 static (`booking`, `transaction`), `streams_to_discover`-selected. No parent/child. `transaction` does its **own request loop**: iterates 9 `TRANSACTION_TYPES` × a rolling 2-month date window (API max), overriding `request_records`. (`streams.py`)
- **Incremental:** None (`replication_key=None`); `booking` uses `createdFrom=0…now`; `transaction` window is last-2-months only (not persisted state).
- **Record shaping:** `convert_keys_to_snake_case` on all rows; `convert_to_string` JSON-stringifies many nested fields; `booking` sets `includeTransactions=true`; `transaction` requires `include_field_group=BOOKING` and lifts `trip_uuid/trip_name/trip_purpose` out of `booking_details` into computed columns.
- **Parity risk:** MED-HIGH — multi-request type×date-window fan-out inside one stream, dual paginator styles, and the booking_details→computed-column extraction.

Only Greenhouse, Lever, Bullhorn do real incremental replication; the rest are full-refresh or fixed-window. The meltano_taps clone was left intact. ## redshift → tap-redshift (fork of data.world/monad-inc)
- **Source system:** Amazon Redshift data warehouse (SQL DB).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** SQL via `psycopg2` (Postgres wire protocol) — `singer-python` + `psycopg2` (`setup.py`). Not SQLAlchemy. Discovery from `information_schema` / `pg_*`; per-stream reads go through a **staging CTAS table** built with `ROW_NUMBER() OVER (...)` then paged. `__init__.py`: `build_stage_ctas`, `psycopg2.connect(...)` (L775), keyset pagination `WHERE key &gt; :last ORDER BY key LIMIT n` with `LIMIT/OFFSET` fallback (`resolve_unique_key`, `fetch_page_with_reconnect`).
- **Auth:** DB credentials — `host/port/user/password` (basic DB login; sslmode configurable).
- **Pagination:** N/A (SQL) — internally keyset (`key &gt; last … LIMIT`) or LIMIT/OFFSET over a staging table.
- **Streams:** SQL-catalog, but **explicit table allowlist** via `tables:` config (array of `{table_name, db_name, schema}`); multi-database/schema. Stream name = `{schema}_{table}`.
- **Incremental/replication:** per-table `replication_key` → INCREMENTAL (bookmark, staging ordered ASC on key) else FULL_TABLE (default; staging window has no ORDER BY).
- **Record shaping:** minimal — column passthrough; views get columns via `SELECT * … LIMIT 0`. No flatten/denorm.
- **Parity risk:** MEDIUM — a generic Redshift/Postgres SQL connector reproduces it, BUT the CTAS-staging + keyset scheme and per-run temp table are non-standard; loader is `overwrite:true` (full-refresh table swap) + `denormalized` + `cluster_on_key_properties`.

## syteline → tap-syteline (custom, singer-sdk)
- **Source system:** Infor SyteLine / CloudSuite Industrial (ERP) via **Infor ION API gateway** (IDOs).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** REST-JSON over ION `IDORequestService` — `GET {ion_base_url}/{tenant}/CSI/IDORequestService/ido/load/{IDO}` with `properties`, `filter`, `orderBy`, `recordCap` params; envelope `{Items, Bookmark, MoreRowsExist, Success}` (`base.py`, `req.py`). Report streams use a stored-proc `clm`/`clmParam` on the same IDO endpoint.
- **Auth:** OAuth2 **password grant** with Infor `.ionapi` service-account keys (SAAK/SASK as username/password + client_id/secret) at `mingle-sso .../{tenant}/as/token.oauth2` (`auth.py`). Bearer token, ~7200s, re-minted.
- **Pagination:** **keyset filter** on the replication/PK column (`key &gt; last AND key &lt;= upper`), server ignores its own Bookmark; two-level (sequential checkpointed batches × parallel PK-range workers, 4 workers).
- **Streams:** **static, allow-listed** (`streams_to_discover` intersect 21 hardcoded classes). Two keyset modes: int PK (`TransNum`) and datetime (`RecordDate`, ms precision). 2 report streams (trial_balance_report, inventory_cost_report) are FULL_TABLE snapshots.
- **Incremental/replication:** INCREMENTAL on `replication_key` (int `TransNum` or datetime `RecordDate`); reports = FULL_TABLE (no PK/rep key).
- **Record shaping:** `_clean_row` drops `_`-prefixed Mongoose internals; type coercion (int/date/numeric field sets); datetime→ISO 8601. No key rename/flatten.
- **Parity risk:** HIGH — keyset pagination against IDO `filter` grammar, ION password-grant, parallel range workers, and report stored-proc (`clm`) calls are hard for a generic REST connector; loader is `upsert:true` + `dedupe_before_upsert`.

## sap → tap-sap (custom, singer-sdk RESTStream)
- **Source system:** SAP Business One **Service Layer** (ERP).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **OData v2** REST-JSON at `https://{host}:{port}/b1s/v1/{EntitySet}`, `records_jsonpath=$.value[*]`; schema discovered from **`$metadata` EDMX** parsed with `xmltodict` (`streams.py`).
- **Auth:** **session-login/cookie** — `POST /b1s/v1/Login` with `CompanyDB/UserName/Password` → `B1SESSION` cookie (+ `CompanyDB` cookie) on every request (module-level session cache).
- **Pagination:** **OData `@odata.nextLink`** (both `odata.nextLink` and `@odata.nextLink` keys); `Prefer: odata.maxpagesize` from `page_size` (default 500).
- **Streams:** user-supplied `entities_to_sync` (comma list, optional `Entity:alias` rename); schema per entity from `$metadata`. PKs from EDMX `&lt;Key&gt;`.
- **Incremental/replication:** **FULL_TABLE only** (`replication_key = None`).
- **Record shaping (IMPORTANT):** records are **flattened** (nested dict → `parent_key_child`) AND **all keys snake_cased** (`standardize_data` = `flatten_dict` + `convert_keys_to_snake_case`); schema property names flattened/snake-cased to match. BigQuery columns must match this transformed shape.
- **Parity risk:** HIGH — session-cookie login, `$metadata`-driven schema, and especially the flatten+snake_case record rewrite mean a plain OData connector would emit different column names; loader `overwrite:true`.

## oracle_fusion → tap-oracle-fusion (custom, singer-sdk)
- **Source system:** Oracle Fusion Cloud (ERP) **BI Publisher** reports.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **SOAP/XML** — `POST {base_url}/xmlpserver/services/v2/ReportService`, op `runReport`, hand-built SOAP envelope (`base.py`). Response is a **base64-encoded XLSX** inside `&lt;reportBytes&gt;`, decoded and parsed with **openpyxl** (headers at row index 4).
- **Auth:** **Basic auth** — username/password embedded in the SOAP `&lt;userID&gt;/&lt;password&gt;` body.
- **Pagination:** none per call (`sizeOfDataChunkDownload=-1` = whole report); iterated by **period window** instead.
- **Streams:** **single hardcoded stream** `trial_balance_mis_report` pointing at one custom `.xdo` report path (`/Custom/Financials/.../Consolidated TB Input File - MIS Report.xdo`); one SOAP call **per monthly period** from `start_date`→today.
- **Incremental/replication:** FULL_TABLE (no replication key); re-runs all periods each time. Hardcoded params (e.g. ledger `"AHS Primary Ledger"`, `P_PERIOD_TYPE=YTD`).
- **Record shaping:** XLSX columns → snake_case header names; all values cast to string; computed `period` column injected per period.
- **Parity risk:** VERY HIGH — SOAP `runReport` + base64 XLSX + openpyxl parsing + tenant-specific report path/params + period-loop. Not reproducible by any generic REST/SQL/file connector; effectively a bespoke report extractor. Loader `overwrite:true`.

## ms365fno → tap-dynamics-finance (custom, singer-sdk)
- **Source system:** Microsoft Dynamics 365 **Finance &amp; Operations** (ERP).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **OData v4** REST-JSON at `{base_url}/data/{EntitySet}` (or `https://{subdomain}.operations.dynamics.com/data`), `records_jsonpath=$.value[*]`; schema from **`$metadata` EDMX** via `xmltodict` (`client.py`).
- **Auth:** **OAuth2 client_credentials** (Azure AD, `resource`=base_url) — or `refresh_token` grant if configured (`auth.py`, SingletonMeta).
- **Pagination:** **`@odata.nextLink`** server-driven; `Prefer: odata.maxpagesize=10000`; `cross-company=true`, optional `dataAreaId eq '{company}'`.
- **Streams:** user-supplied `entities_to_sync` (comma list → `GenericDynamicsStream`); stream name snake_cased; schema/PKs from `$metadata`. Optional **parallel PK-range partitioning** for FULL_TABLE streams (`partitioned_streams`, 4–8 workers) + source `$count` **reconciliation**.
- **Incremental/replication:** INCREMENTAL on **`SysModifiedDateTime`** (`{key} gt/lt {ts}` `$filter`) when the entity exposes it, else FULL_TABLE.
- **Record shaping:** **records pass through unchanged** (`post_process` only counts keys) — OData field names kept as-is; only stream *names* snake_cased.
- **Parity risk:** MEDIUM-HIGH — core is standard OData-v4-with-nextLink (reproducible), but AAD client-creds, `$metadata` schema, `cross-company`, SysModified incremental, and the PK-range parallel partitioning + `$count` recon are advanced; loader `upsert:true` + `dedupe_before_upsert` + `use_stream_name_as_table_name`.

## ms365bc → tap-dynamics-business-central (custom, singer-sdk; dual-mode)
- **Source system:** Microsoft Dynamics 365 **Business Central** (ERP).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** default = **BC REST API v2.0** JSON at `https://api.businesscentral.dynamics.com/v2.0/{tenant}/{environment}/api/v2.0/companies({id})/{entity}` (`standard_api.py`), schema from `/api/v2.0/$metadata` (xmltodict). Nested lines pulled inline via **`?$expand=&lt;lines&gt;`**. Legacy escape-hatch mode (`use_legacy_odata_v4`) = OData v4 published web services (`client.py`, `GenericDynamicsStream`).
- **Auth:** **OAuth2 client_credentials** (Azure AD, `scope=https://api.businesscentral.dynamics.com/.default`, token at `login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/oauth2/v2.0/token`) (`auth.py`).
- **Pagination:** **`@odata.nextLink`**; `Prefer: odata.maxpagesize` (default 100; GL entries pinned to BC max).
- **Streams:** **fixed catalog** (~18+ `TOP_LEVEL_ENTITIES`: companyInformation, accounts, customers, vendors, contacts, items, employees, dimensions, dimensionValues, attachments, generalLedgerEntries, journals, sales/purchase invoices/orders/creditMemos, payment journals, taxGroups, balanceSheets, cashFlowStatements, …). **Fans out across environments × companies** (enumerated via `GET /companies`); `entities_to_sync` filters the catalog. **Parent→child fan-out:** `journal_lines` is a separate flat stream requiring per-(env,company,journal) enumeration then `/journals({id})/journalLines` per journal. Beta `reportsFinance/beta` namespace is opt-in.
- **Incremental/replication:** INCREMENTAL only for `generalLedgerEntries` (`lastModifiedDateTime`); everything else FULL_TABLE. GL also supports optional `accountNumber`/`postingDate` range `$filter`s.
- **Record shaping (IMPORTANT):** **all keys snake_cased recursively** (incl. `$expand`-ed nested line arrays); each record annotated with injected `company_id`, `company_name`, `environment`. Cross-environment schemas unioned into one table shape. 403/404 per (entity,company) downgraded to empty.
- **Parity risk:** VERY HIGH — multi-company/multi-environment fan-out, `$expand` line inlining, journal-lines parent→child enumeration, snake_case + company/env annotation, cross-env schema union, and dual API modes. A generic OData connector cannot reproduce the emitted table shape; loader `upsert:true` + `dedupe_before_upsert` + `use_stream_name_as_table_name`.

**Cross-cutting notes:** all six load to the shared `z3z1ma` target-bigquery with `denormalized` + `schema_resolver_version:"2"` + `add_underscore_when_invalid`; SQL/report/full-table taps (redshift, sap, oracle_fusion) use `overwrite:true` (full-refresh swap), while the incremental ERP taps (syteline, ms365fno, ms365bc) use `upsert:true` + `dedupe_before_upsert`. All five custom taps depend on a private `meltano-utils` (git tag v0) wrapping singer-sdk/requests. `meltano_taps` clone left intact; my g1 clones deleted.</result>

## Summary of all six taps

Analysis complete for all 6. Key cross-cutting finding: **4 of 6 taps read/write BigQuery directly** (netsuite, quickbooks, xero DELETE from BQ; sage/intacct only READS BQ for soft-delete detection — the meltano config's "deletion" settings do **not** mean the tap deletes from BQ). netsuite_pro and salesforce are pure Singer stdout taps. Two "premise corrections" surfaced: netsuite_pro is OData v4 (not report-portal scraping), and tap-intacct does no DML against BQ.

### NetSuite (branch `feature-tap`) — ERP
- **Mechanism:** SuiteQL over SuiteTalk REST — POST `{"q":&lt;SQL&gt;}` to `/query/v1/suiteql` on `{account}.suitetalk.api.netsuite.com/services/rest`; schema via metadata-catalog `/record/v1/metadata-catalog/`. No SOAP/RESTlet (zeep is a dead dep). (`objects/transaction.py:8`, `rest.py:98-101,485-582`)
- **Auth:** OAuth1 TBA, HMAC-SHA256 (consumer+token key/secret). (`rest.py:785-796`)
- **Pagination:** offset `limit=1000` to `hasMore=false`; adaptive ID-batch/date-window halving to dodge 99k offset ceiling. (`rest.py:896-938`, `switchrest.py:225`)
- **Streams:** static `ALL_STREAMS` + dynamic CustomTable; `streams_to_discover` selects. Parent→child: transaction-line streams enumerate changed+deleted txn IDs, refetch lines in 1000-ID `IN()` chunks. (`transaction_line.py:112-206`)
- **Incremental:** "switch" bookmark — ID-window sweep then flips to `lastmodifieddate`/`linelastmodifieddate`. (`switchrest.py:87-107`)
- **Shaping:** mostly pass-through; `MergedTransactionData` is a server-side SuiteQL JOIN+SUM+GROUP BY (computed grain). (`merged_transaction_data.py:36-101`)
- **Tap→BQ: YES.** `utils.cleanup_transactions_in_bq` `DELETE ... WHERE transaction IN UNNEST(@ids)` + reads BQ for deleted-ID resolution, before re-emit; 20k ID chunks, non-atomic. (`utils.py:84-224`, `transaction_line.py:72-77`)
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** stateful destination mutator; SuiteQL dialect + offset ceiling; non-idempotent fan-out; JOIN/SUM grain.

### NetSuite Pro — SuiteProjects Pro published reports
- **Mechanism:** OData v4 JSON REST — `GET /reports/{report_id}` on `{subdomain}.app.netsuitesuiteprojectspro.com/odata/v4`, records at `$.value[*]`. (README's OAuth2 claim is stale.) (`streams.py:24-64`)
- **Auth:** HTTP Basic, `base64(company_name\user:password)`. (`auth.py:15-18`)
- **Pagination:** OData `@odata.nextLink` → `$skiptoken` + `$top=1000`. (`streams.py:107-123`)
- **Streams:** dynamic — one stream per `reports` CSV entry (`report_id:report_name`). (`tap.py:51-66`)
- **Incremental:** NONE — full snapshot each run.
- **Shaping:** keys snake_cased; schema inferred from `$top=1`, all columns nullable string.
- **Tap→BQ: NO.** Standard stdout tap.
- **Parity risk:** low; all-string schema, no incremental, `split(":")` fragility.

### Salesforce — CRM (Stitch fork v1.9.0)
- **Mechanism:** `api_type: BULK` → Bulk API v1 (async job/batch, SOQL) — `POST /services/async/60.0/job` (queryAll, CSV), batch with SOQL, 20s poll, stream CSV. SOQL: `SELECT … WHERE &lt;replkey&gt; &gt;= &lt;start&gt; ORDER BY &lt;replkey&gt; ASC`. Bulk2/REST present but inactive. 12-worker concurrency. (`salesforce/bulk.py:44,163-312`, `__init__.py:591-622`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 refresh_token (re-login 900s); password+security-token fallback. (`credentials.py:63-113`)
- **Pagination:** async job/batch; PK-chunking (50k) fallback only on timeout/too-large. (`bulk.py:110-161`)
- **Streams:** catalog-driven, discovered via `describe` (composite, batches of 25); `streams_to_discover` defaults to ALL sobjects in-repo; bulk-incompatible objects blacklisted.
- **Incremental:** SystemModstamp → LastModifiedDate → CreatedDate; PK-chunked jobs resume via JobID/BatchIDs state.
- **Shaping:** passthrough (drop `attributes`, `""`→None, anyType coercion).
- **Tap→BQ: NO.**
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** Bulk-v1 async lifecycle + XML parse, PK-chunking + resumable state, SOQL generation, queryAll-includes-deleted, 12-way concurrency, pinned v60.0.

### QuickBooks (branch `feature-tap`) — QBO accounting (hotglue fork v1.4.54)
- **Mechanism:** QBO REST v3 — entities `GET /query?query=SELECT * FROM &lt;E&gt; STARTPOSITION n MAXRESULTS m` (minorversion 45); reports `GET /reports/&lt;Name&gt;` (minorversion 75). Host `/v3/company/&lt;realmId&gt;`. (`rest.py:34-168`, `rest_reports.py:97-103`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 refresh_token (Intuit bearer, refresh 3300s, token rotated); `realmId` in path.
- **Pagination:** entities offset `STARTPOSITION`/`MAXRESULTS` (ladder 1000→50, `ORDERBY Id ASC`), separate deleted pass; reports date-windowed.
- **Streams:** entities from `QB_OBJECTS` filtered by `streams_to_discover`; 8 static report classes.
- **Incremental:** entities INCREMENTAL on `MetaData.LastUpdatedTime`; reports FULL_TABLE + rolling `incremental_lookback_days` window (730 default).
- **Shaping:** report Rows/Columns matrix flattened via `_recursive_row_search` (Categories path, ColData→titled cols, computed `SyncTimestampUtc`); `gl_basic_fields` reduces columns.
- **Tap→BQ: YES.** `bigquery.py::delete_rolling_window()` + `truncate_table()`, called from `_delete_report_window`→`query_report` before yielding, driven by `REPORT_DELETE_STRATEGY`. (`__init__.py:752-803,939`)
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** heuristic matrix flattening (minorversion-fragile); tap mutating BQ (wrong date_column dups/over-deletes; non-BQ targets uncleaned); offset dup/skip; date-window sizing changes emitted rows.

### Xero (branch `feature-tap`) — accounting (v2.3.2)
- **Mechanism:** Xero Accounting REST (`api.xero.com/api.xro/2.0/&lt;Endpoint&gt;`, JSON); Reports `/reports/ProfitAndLoss`,`/BalanceSheet`; separate Projects API base. (`client.py:20,312`, `streams.py:210-213`)
- **Auth:** OAuth2 refresh_token (`identity.xero.com/connect/token`) + `Xero-tenant-id` header; rotated token → `/tmp/refresh_token.txt`.
- **Pagination:** page-based `?page&amp;pageSize=1000` (most); Journals offset on `JournalNumber`; reports month-windowed. (`streams.py:255-350,532-642`)
- **Streams:** static classes filtered by `streams_to_discover`; parent→child in `ProjectChildStream` (`/Projects/{id}/Tasks|Time`).
- **Incremental:** `UpdatedDateUTC` via `If-Modified-Since` header (accounts/contacts/tracking_categories exempt=full pull); Journals via offset; reports rolling `incremental_lookback_days` (730).
- **Shaping:** `transform.py` prunes/denormalizes (JSON-stringify nested LineItems/Payments); report matrix flattened inline → row per (account×month) `{from_date,to_date,account,value}`. (`streams.py:568-635`)
- **Tap→BQ: YES.** `bigquery.py::delete_rolling_window()` `DELETE ... WHERE DATE(&lt;col&gt;) &gt;= @cutoff`, from `Reports._begin_rolling_window` on non-first sync (P&amp;L + balance sheet only). (`bigquery.py:71-135`, `streams.py:436-522`)
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** tap DELETEs BQ (table name must match stream name); ~730d revisit window; header-cell date-label parsing (locale drift); If-Modified-Since reliance; token persisted only to `/tmp`.

### Sage Intacct (`tap-intacct`) — ERP accounting
- **Mechanism:** Sage Intacct XML Gateway — HTTP POST XML (`xmltodict.unparse`) to `api.intacct.com/ia/xml/xmlgw.phtml`, using the **modern `query` function** (object/select/filter/pagesize/offset/orderby). `readByQuery` only in `get_sample`; **no `readMore`/resultId chain** (deliberately replaced). (`client.py:635,733-743,908-910`)
- **Auth:** Session-token (NOT HMAC) — `control` sender_id/password + login userid/companyid/password → `getAPISession` returns sessionid; 6-session pool per worker. (`client.py:222-337`)
- **Pagination:** offset `pagesize=2000` ordered by RECORDNO; GLENTRY/GLBATCH use bounded RECORDNO windows (100k) with concurrent page fetch; total from `@totalcount`. (`client.py:648-819`)
- **Streams:** dynamically discovered schemas via `lookup`; static `INTACCT_OBJECTS` map, `streams_to_discover` selects (default class/department); `audit_history` fans out per object; `additional_batches_to_sync` for extra GL batches. (`const.py:100-164`, `__init__.py:171-270,744-755`)
- **Incremental:** bookmark `WHENMODIFIED` via `greaterthanorequalto` filter (`REP_KEYS` overrides; some full-table); GL windows on RECORDNO but bookmark on rep key; state every 25k.
- **Shaping:** XML→dict, keys `.`→`_` lowercased; `exclude_fields` drops columns; synthetic `is_deleted` injected into every record/schema. No in-tap joins.
- **Tap→BQ: READ-ONLY (premise correction).** `bigquery.py` runs only `SELECT` — reads rolling-window RECORDNOs from BQ (`gl_entry_deletion_lookback_days`/`gl_batch_deletion_lookback_days`, 730 default), set-diffs vs Sage to detect Sage-side deletes, refetches those rows from BQ and **re-emits them as normal Singer records with `is_deleted=True`**. No DELETE/DML anywhere. (`bigquery.py:40-127`, `glentry.py:60`, `__init__.py:598-643`)
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** proprietary XML-gateway query + windowed/offset pagination + `@totalcount` sizing; multi-session-pool auth + Intacct concurrency caps; tap **reads back its own prior BQ output** to compute soft-deletes (couples tap to destination table shape; skips detection if BQ config absent); synthetic `is_deleted` semantics.</result>

## BambooHR (`tap_bamboohr/streams.py`, `tap.py`, `utils.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Source/category:** BambooHR — HRIS.
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on the Meltano Singer SDK (`RESTStream`). Base `https://api.bamboohr.com/api/gateway.php/{subdomain}/v1`. Custom-report schema inference + `/meta/fields` metadata use raw `requests.get` against a *different* host `https://{subdomain}.bamboohr.com/api/v1/...` (`utils.py:12`).
- **Auth:** API key as HTTP **Basic username**, password hardcoded `"foobar"` (`BasicAuthenticator`, `streams.py:55`).
- **Pagination:** Mostly single-page; **CustomReport** uses page-number paginator (500/page, `page=n`, advances while `pagination.next_page` non-null).
- **Streams:** Static registry (not discovered). 4 core (Employees, CompensationHistory, EmploymentHistoryStatus, JobInfo) + opt-in `additional_streams` (CSV → `STREAM_MAPPING`) + `custom_reports` (`"31:employee_report"` pairs). **Custom reports:** active path is **GET** `/custom-reports/{report_id}`, records at `$.data[*]`, schema inferred at construction from a raw GET (all cols typed `["null","string"]`); a legacy unused class does **POST** `/reports/custom`. `include_fields`/`exclude_fields` apply only to Employees (with `id:alias` renaming) driven by `/meta/fields`.
- **Incremental:** **Full-table** everywhere (`replication_key=None`); passes fixed `since=2012-01-01` / `1900..2100` to pull all history. Composite PKs.
- **Shaping (heavy):** camelCase→snake_case recursive, temporal-null cleanup, string→bool coercion; explodes `{employee_id:{rows:[…]}}` envelope into one row/entry; BigQuery-safe field-name normalization; TimeOffRequests flatten; Photos base64-encode.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** runtime per-report schema inference; two API hosts + `/meta/fields` join; parent→child per-employee fan-out (`GET /employees/{id}`); map-of-object envelope explode (non-standard JSONPath); `foobar` Basic auth; field alias/normalization logic.

## ADP (`tap_adp/authenticator.py`, `client.py`, `tap.py`, `streams.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Source/category:** ADP (Workforce Now, incl. Brazil) — payroll/HCM. **EU host** `https://api.eu.adp.com` (not US).
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK (`ADPStream(RESTStream)`). Endpoints `/hr/v2/workers`, `/payroll/v1|v2/...`, `/benefits/v1|v2/...`, `/staffing/v1|v2/...`, `/hcm/v1/validation-tables/departments`.
- **Auth — mTLS CONFIRMED:** Dual layer. OAuth2 **client_credentials** to `/auth/oauth/v2/token` (scope `read`) **+ client-certificate mutual TLS on both the token request and every data request**. `cert_public`/`cert_private` arrive as base64 PEM in config, decoded to temp files `/var/tmp/adp_certs/client_cert.crt` + `client_key.pem` (`tap.py:76`); token POST uses `requests.post(..., cert=(pub,priv))` (`authenticator.py:31`); data session sets `session.cert=(pub,priv)`, `verify=True` (`client.py:70`). Bearer is obtained *over* the mTLS channel; both bearer + cert presented on data calls.
- **Pagination:** `$top=100`/`$skip` **offset**, stops on **HTTP 204** (`has_more = status_code != 204`).
- **Streams:** Static list (15 classes), filtered by `streams_to_sync` CSV (default `workers,pay_statement,benefit_coverage,payroll_output,benefit_offering`). Parent→child per-worker fan-out via `associate_oid`.
- **Incremental:** **Full-table only** (`replication_key=None`; the one incremental filter is commented out). `start_date` effectively unused.
- **Shaping (heavy):** recursive snake_case, flatten_dict, "first element of list" denormalization (work_assignments etc.), computed department-alias columns, composite PKs from nested `nameCode.code`, `Decimal` float parsing; custom skippable-error handling for ADP 404/400 "still loading" codes + aggressive backoff.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** the mTLS story (cert on *both* token + data; reqwest `Identity`); 204-terminated offset paging; per-worker N+1 joins; take-first denormalization + computed cols; error-swallowing.

## iSolved (`tap_isolved/auth.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`)
- **Source/category:** iSolved — HCM/payroll.
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK. Base `{api_url}/rest/api`; records at `$.results[*]`.
- **Auth:** OAuth2 **client_credentials**; token endpoint `{api_url}/rest/api/token`; credentials sent as an **HTTP Basic header** (not form body), `grant_type=client_credentials` form-urlencoded.
- **Pagination:** page-number; fixed `pageSize=500`; next page derived by **URL-parsing `nextPageUrl`'s `page` query param** and re-injecting as `page`.
- **Streams:** Static (`client`, `employee`). Parent→child: employees enumerated **per client** (`/clients/{_client_id}/employees`, one call per client id).
- **Incremental:** **Full-table only** (`replication_key=None`), PK `id`.
- **Shaping:** per-row `flatten_dict` + `convert_keys_to_snake_case`; no joins/computed cols.
- **Parity risk:** per-client fan-out (needs `${client.id}` templating); Basic-header client_credentials; the parse-`page`-out-of-`nextPageUrl` pagination quirk (plain "follow next URL" would break).

## HiBob (`tap_hibob/client.py`, `streams/*.py`, `utils.py`, `constants.py`)
- **Source/category:** HiBob ("bob") — HRIS.
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK (`HibobStream(RESTStream)`). Base `https://api.hibob.com`. Mix of **POST search** (`/v1/people/search`, `/v1/objects/position/search`, `/v1/positions/*/search`) and **GET bulk** (`/v1/bulk/people/employment|salaries|work`). Position streams do a **GET metadata** call (`/v1/metadata/objects/position`) before each search; sidecar `/v1/company/people/fields` and `/v1/company/named-lists/{list}`.
- **Auth:** HTTP **Basic** (service-user id:token via `BasicAuthenticator`), or optional pre-computed `authorization` API-key header; sidecar calls build the base64 Basic header manually.
- **Pagination:** Mixed — base `page`+`$.next_page` (vestigial); bulk = cursor (`limit=200` + `response_metadata.next_cursor`); positions = body pagination `{"pagination":{"limit":1000,"cursor":…}}`; employee/positions single-page.
- **Streams:** Static; 5 always-on + 3 opt-in via `additional_streams`. No parent→child actually consumed (each stream enumerates all employees). People search is metadata-driven: schema dynamically extended from `/company/people/fields`.
- **Incremental:** **Full-table** everywhere; `start_date` unused.
- **Shaping (heavy):** child-explode of bulk `results[].values[]` stamping parent `employeeId`; dot-path flatten camel→snake; **human-readable ID→label joins** via named-lists (department/title/employment_type/work_change_type); `column_N` custom-field rename; position `humanReadable` cells + `_Id` twin columns; date normalization; `synced_at`.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** POST-search bodies (`showInactive`, `fields`, `filters`, embedded pagination); per-tenant metadata-driven dynamic columns; per-record named-list label joins (extra endpoints); two different cursor schemes; dual auth; `humanReadable`/`_Id` twin columns and PK backfill.

## Paylocity (`tap_paylocity/base.py`, `streams.py`, `schemas/*`)
- **Source/category:** Paylocity — payroll/HRIS.
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK (`RESTStream`). Base `https://api.paylocity.com/api`. List `GET /v2/companies/{company_id}/employees`, detail `GET /v2/companies/{company_id}/employees/{employeeId}`; `parse_response` handles bare arrays and `{employees|data:[…]}` wrappers.
- **Auth:** OAuth2 **client_credentials** (custom authenticator); token `https://api.paylocity.com/IdentityServer/connect/token`, fixed **`scope=WebLinkAPI`**; requires `client_id`/`client_secret`/`company_id`.
- **Pagination:** custom page-number (`pagesize=100`+`pagenumber`), continues while returned list ≥ page_size; detail endpoint unpaginated.
- **Streams:** Static classes (`employee_list`, `employee`), gated by `streams_to_discover` CSV. **Parent→child fan-out:** `employee` detail called **once per employee** (`employeeId` from parent context).
- **Incremental:** **Full-table only** (`replication_key=None`), PK `employee_id`.
- **Shaping:** camel→snake; `flatten_keys` denormalizes ~13 nested objects into ~150 flat `parent_child` columns; arrays kept.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** N+1 detail fan-out; non-standard IdentityServer token endpoint + `WebLinkAPI` scope + `company_id` in path; length-≥-page_size pagination heuristic (extra empty request on exact multiples); dual wrapped/bare response handling.

## Personio (`tap_personio/auth.py`, `base.py`, `streams.py`, `constants.py`, `utils.py`)
- **Source/category:** Personio — HRIS.
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK. Base `https://api.personio.de`. `/v1/company/employees`, `/v1/company/employees/custom-attributes`, `/v2/persons/{employee_id}/employments`, v2 reports `/v2/reports/{uuid}` (+ `/v2/reports/attributes`), v1 custom reports `/v1/company/custom-reports/reports/{uuid}`. (No attendances stream.)
- **Auth:** custom client-credentials — POST `{client_id,client_secret}` JSON to `/v1/auth`, token read from `data.data.token` with `success` flag → `Authorization: Bearer`. **Token rotation:** refreshed if within 1h of expiry; **singleton token manager** shared across streams.
- **Pagination:** v1 = offset/limit (`PersonioOffsetPaginator`, 200); v2 = cursor via `_meta.links.next.href` (50); v1 custom reports = single-page.
- **Streams:** 2 static (employees, employments) + **dynamically instantiated report streams** from `reports` / `v1_reports` config (`uuid:name` pairs). Parent→child: employments per-employee (`/v2/persons/{employee_id}/employments`).
- **Incremental:** **Full-table** everywhere (`replication_key=None`).
- **Shaping (heavy):** unwrap Personio `{value:X}` attribute envelope; `dynamic_*`→`customfield_&lt;label&gt;` rename (needs custom-attributes call); currency `_currency` companion cols; **runtime schema inference** with integer→number widening; employments nested flatten; v1-custom-report **pivot** per-attribute history into one row per `(employee_id, snapshot_date)`; date→ISO `T00:00:00Z`.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** non-RFC auth envelope + 1h-early refresh + singleton cache; runtime schema inference (int→number, date heuristics) or BQ loads diverge; `{value}` unwrap + second API call for renames; three different report modes incl. lossy v1 pivot; full-refresh only. Note: `meltano.yml` `streams_to_discover` key is stale (tap only accepts `reports`/`v1_reports`).

## UKG Pro (`tap_ukg_pro/base.py`, `streams.py`, `mappings.py`, `constants.py`; branch feature-tap)
- **Source/category:** UKG Pro (UltiPro) — HCM/payroll. Stream names deliberately match existing BigQuery tables (Merge-parity replacement).
- **Mechanism:** REST-JSON on Meltano SDK (`UkgProStream(DriveRestStream)`). **Multiple REST surfaces** on one host: `/personnel/v1/*`, `/configuration/v1/*`, `/payroll/v1/*` (incl. **POST** `companies/pay-statements`). `normalize_base_url` strips the SOAP `.../services/BIDataService` suffix customers paste; `validate_response` rejects non-JSON 200 (SOAP-host misconfig guard).
- **Auth:** HTTP **Basic (username/password) + `US-Customer-Api-Key` header** (`customer_api_key`); HTTPS enforced.
- **Pagination:** 1-indexed page-number, params `page` + **`per_Page`** (capital P per UKG spec); stops on short page or `MAX_PAGES=1000`; unwraps `data`/`results`/`items` envelopes.
- **Streams:** Static list, config-filtered — `STREAM_TYPES` = 10 core + 7 extended; `streams_to_discover` empty = 10 Merge-parity core, `"all"` = 17, else CSV allowlist (raises on unknown). `team_org_level` selects which org level becomes `team`; `sync_user_defined_fields` drives per-company UDF fetch.
- **Parent→child / enumeration:** companies cached from company-details; UDFs fetched **per company** (`/companies/{id}/user-defined-fields`); `employeepayrollrun` POSTs full `companyIds` list; `Employment` **cross-joins** job-history against cached compensation/employment indexes.
- **Incremental:** Mixed — `employee` (`modified_at`→`dateTimeChanged`), `payrollrun` (`check_date`), `employeepayrollrun` (`checkDateFrom` in POST body) are incremental (`is_sorted=False`, server filter `&gt;MM-DD-YYYY`); all others full-table. `start_date` seeds first run.
- **Shaping (heavy):** `mappings.py` reshapes to fixed Merge-compatible non-minimal column lists (drop → emit None to avoid COLUMN_MISSING); enum normalization to Merge vocab; **`org_level_type_map` config values become downstream BigQuery column names**; `org_level_group_id = f"L{level}_{code}"`; UDFs injected into both SCHEMA and RECORD; computed `pay_rate` float / composite `employment_id`; earnings/deductions JSON-stringified; extended streams just snake_case.
- **Parity risk (HIGH):** Basic + custom `US-Customer-Api-Key` header; **`org_level_type_map`** (config-seeded values → BQ column names; wrong map silently renames/drops columns); multi-surface + SOAP-URL stripping + `per_Page` quirk + envelope variance; UDFs must land in the SCHEMA message or the target drops them; POST-body pagination for `employeepayrollrun`; `is_sorted=False` incremental depends on ISO-normalized keys; heavy in-tap cross-joins.

---

### Cross-cutting observations
- **All 7 are REST-JSON built on the Meltano/Singer SDK** (`RESTStream`). None use GraphQL, SOAP, or file/report-download transport (UKG explicitly *avoids* its SOAP `BIDataService` surface; BambooHR custom reports are JSON over REST, not file downloads).
- **ADP is the sole mTLS case** — client cert on both token and data requests, over the EU host. This is the single biggest parity blocker.
- **Replication is overwhelmingly full-table** (BambooHR, ADP, iSolved, HiBob, Paylocity, Personio all full-refresh); **only UKG Pro** has genuine incremental streams (employee/payrollrun/employeepayrollrun). This matches the `overwrite: true` target-bigquery config on most (ADP uses `upsert`; UKG's meltano.yml explicitly comments that overwrite is an invariant so terminated employees don't inflate headcount).
- **Parent→child per-entity fan-out (N+1) is pervasive:** ADP (per-worker), iSolved (per-client), Paylocity (per-employee), Personio (per-employee), UKG (per-company UDFs), BambooHR (per-employee + custom reports).
- **Auth is non-standard in almost every case:** BambooHR `apikey:foobar` Basic; iSolved Basic-header client_credentials; Paylocity IdentityServer + `WebLinkAPI` scope; Personio custom `/v1/auth` JSON envelope; UKG Basic + custom API-key header; ADP mTLS+OAuth. A generic OAuth2/Basic provider will not cover them without per-source customization.
- **Heavy in-tap record shaping everywhere** (camel→snake, flatten, denormalize/explode, human-readable label joins, dynamic/runtime schema inference, computed columns) — a plain faucet REST source would need substantial transform/mapping work to reach parity, and the dynamic-schema cases (BambooHR custom reports, HiBob metadata columns, Personio reports, UKG UDFs + org-level column naming) are the hardest to reproduce faithfully.

The `meltano_taps` clone was left intact; fresh clones live under `.../scratchpad/g4/`.</result>
