## tap-shopify
Files: `tap_shopify/base.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`, `queries.py`, `constants.py`
- **Source/category:** Shopify — e-commerce platform (orders/customers/products/analytics).
- **Mechanism (EXACT): GraphQL — Shopify Admin GraphQL API** (confirmed; no REST). Endpoint `{store_url}/admin/api/{version}/graphql.json` (base.py L64-66; version `2026-01`, constants.py). All streams subclass `singer_sdk.GraphQLStream`; POST `{query, variables}`. Two flavors: Admin GraphQL connections + **ShopifyQL** analytics via the `shopifyqlQuery` field (a proprietary SQL-like DSL).
- **Auth:** Static Admin API token in header `X-Shopify-Access-Token` (base.py L68-75). Not an OAuth runtime flow.
- **Pagination:** (1) GraphQL cursor `pageInfo.hasNextPage`/`endCursor` → `$after` for Admin streams; (2) LIMIT/OFFSET for ShopifyQL streams. PAGE_SIZE=250.
- **Streams:** Static list of 5 (`ALL_STREAMS`), config-gated by `streams_to_discover` (not live discovery). Child objects (line items, refunds, fulfillments, variants) fetched **inline as nested sub-connections**, not child streams.
- **Incremental:** INCREMENTAL. Admin: key `updatedAt`, applied as GraphQL search string `query:"updated_at:&gt;=&lt;cutoff&gt;"` + `sortKey:UPDATED_AT`. ShopifyQL: key `day` via `SINCE &lt;date&gt;`.
- **Record shaping:** edges→node unwrap; `flatten_keys` → `parent_child` columns; nested arrays JSON-stringified; ShopifyQL synthesizes composite `unique_identifier` keys.
- **Parity risk: HIGH** — GraphQL cursors + Shopify query DSL filter; proprietary ShopifyQL DSL; custom THROTTLED rate-limit handling; lossy opinionated post_process; capped nested sub-connections (no inner pagination).

## tap-zohobooks
Files: `base.py`, `auth.py`, `tap.py`, `bigquery.py`, `streams.py`, `constants.py`
- **Source/category:** Zoho Books — cloud accounting SaaS.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): REST-JSON.** Base `https://www.zohoapis.&lt;region&gt;/books/v3`; transactional paths (`/invoices`, `/bills`, `/contacts`, `/journals/{id}`) plus **report endpoints** (`/reports/profitandloss`, `/balancesheet`, `/cashflow`, `/accounttransaction`, `/trialbalance`). `bigquery.py` does **not** read source data — it reaches into the **destination** BQ for a rolling-window DELETE of report rows and landed-PK lookups.
- **Auth:** OAuth2 refresh_token grant at `accounts.zoho.&lt;region&gt;/oauth/v2/token`; non-standard header `Authorization: Zoho-oauthtoken &lt;token&gt;`; region auto-probed; refresh-token rotation persisted to `/tmp`.
- **Pagination:** Page-number (`page_context.has_more_page`), `per_page=200`; special paginators for `/chartofaccounts` (has_more bug) and reports.
- **Streams:** 29 static classes, gated by `streams_to_discover` (parents auto-included). Heavy parent→child fan-out: `invoices→invoice_details` etc. (one GET per parent id); `chart_of_accounts→gl_accounts→account_transactions`.
- **Incremental:** Key `last_modified_time` → `?last_modified_time=&lt;iso&gt;` on most; journals use `date_after`; reports have no watermark → replayed via `report_lookback_days` (default 730d).
- **Record shaping:** Heavy — nested objects/arrays JSON-stringified to match legacy Skyvia schema; `*_details` per-id explosions; account-id harvesting mixin across streams; reports stamped with synthetic `gl_line_key` sha256 PK.
- **Parity risk: HIGH** — non-standard auth header + token rotation to `/tmp` + external drive PATCH; 8-region auto-probe; **warehouse dependency mid-sync** (BQ DELETE + landed-key reads); aggressive N+1 fan-out joins; strict stream ordering; fragile rate-limit handling.

## tap-invoiced
Files: `base.py`, `client.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`
- **Source/category:** Invoiced — A/R billing &amp; invoicing SaaS.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): REST-JSON.** `url_base = https://api.invoiced.com`; GET `/customers`, `/invoices`, `/payments`, `/items`, `/subscriptions`.
- **Auth:** HTTP Basic — api_key as username, empty password (`session.auth = (api_key, "")`, client.py L49).
- **Pagination:** **Page-number** (not Link-header, despite common assumption) — `page` + `per_page=100`; custom loop stops when a returned array has `&lt; per_page` records (base.py L150-205).
- **Streams:** 5 static classes, filtered by `streams_to_discover`. No parent→child fan-out.
- **Incremental:** Key `updated_at` on every stream; `sort=updated_at asc` + `updated_after` (ISO→epoch).
- **Record shaping:** Epoch→ISO conversion on date fields; per-stream nested objects JSON-stringified. No joins/explode/computed cols.
- **Parity risk: MODERATE** — custom page-loop assumes bare JSON array and stops on short page (exact-multiple-of-100 edge); Basic-auth-with-api-key-as-username; epoch↔ISO conversion on both request and record.

## tap-restaurant365
Files: `base.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`, `constants.py`
- **Source/category:** Restaurant365 — restaurant/hospitality accounting &amp; ops ERP.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): OData — but v4 JSON envelope on the wire, despite the code/README labeling it "OData v2".** Fixed service URL `https://odata.restaurant365.net/api/v2/views/{EntitySet}` (constants.py). Records from `$.value[*]`, pagination from `@odata.nextLink` (the **v4** envelope — no `d`/`results` anywhere). Query params: `$filter`, `$orderby`, `$skip`; deliberately **no `$top`** (suppresses nextLink), no `$select`.
- **Auth:** HTTP Basic; username store-qualified as `&lt;store_name&gt;\&lt;username&gt;` (backslash).
- **Pagination:** OData `@odata.nextLink` with `$skip` offset (server-fixed page 5000); plus a bespoke `DateWindowPaginator` sliding 30-day windows with narrow-on-timeout.
- **Streams:** 20 hardcoded static streams; no fan-out/enumeration. Five filtered `/Transaction` streams via `type eq '...'`. Dedicated `entity_deleted` tombstone stream (`/EntityDeleted`, key `deletedOn`).
- **Incremental:** `$filter &lt;key&gt; ge &lt;bookmark&gt; and &lt;key&gt; lt &lt;end&gt;` + `$orderby`. Key `modifiedOn` mostly; `deletedOn` for deletions; `payroll_summary` has none (full replay). 30-day windowing on sales/transaction/payroll.
- **Record shaping:** Minimal (no flatten/join). `modifiedOn` normalized to 3 fractional digits; `transaction`/`transaction_detail` separate, detail keyed on composite `[transactionDetailId, rowType]`; payroll null-PK coalesce to `""`.
- **Parity risk: HIGH** — **v4-vs-v2 envelope mismatch trap**; inclusive `ge` sub-second `$filter` date semantics (UTC-only); load-bearing 30-day windowing + narrow-on-500; required `$orderby` tiebreakers for deterministic `$skip`; backslash store-prefixed Basic username.

## tap-skyslope
Files: `base.py`, `client.py`, `bulk.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`, `constants.py`
- **Source/category:** SkySlope — real estate transaction management.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): REST-JSON.** `url_base = https://api.skyslope.com/api`; endpoints `/contacts`, `/users`, `/offices`, `/files/...`. **`bulk.py` is NOT an async bulk/job API** — it's client-side date-range chunking (1-year windows via `createdAfter/Before`, `modifiedAfter/Before`).
- **Auth: HMAC, two-layer.** Layer 1: HMAC-SHA256 over `client_id:client_secret:timestamp` keyed by `secret_key`, base64 → header `Authorization: SS {access_key}:{hmac}` + `Timestamp`. Layer 2: `POST /auth/login` returns a Session token cached until expiry, added as a header.
- **Pagination:** (a) yearly date-range chunking for bulk `/files` streams; (b) **link-header** `links[rel=="next"].href` for detail index endpoints.
- **Streams:** 8 static, config-filtered. `listings`/`sales` use bulk date-range; `listings_detail`/`sales_detail` are **parent→child fan-out** (enumerate GUIDs from index, then GET `/files/listings/{guid}` — N+1).
- **Incremental:** `listings` key `modified_on`, `sales` key `created_on`; others full-refresh. Detail streams add SHA256-fingerprint per-GUID skip stored in state.
- **Record shaping:** `flatten_dict` + snake_case keys; nested objects JSON-stringified; computed `property_address` from flattened parts; detail streams unwrap `value.&lt;key&gt;` envelope.
- **Parity risk: HIGH** — HMAC signing + separate Session-token login (dual auth, per-request timestamp); client-side 1-year windowing loop; link-header paging; N+1 GUID detail fan-out; stateful fingerprint dedup; module-global rate-limit throttling.

## tap-attio
Files: `base.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`, `constants.py`
- **Source/category:** Attio — CRM.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): REST-JSON over `https://api.attio.com/v2`.** GET for catalog endpoints; **POST-based `.../query` endpoints** for records/entries (`rest_method="POST"`) — `/objects/{id}/records/query`, `/lists/{id}/entries/query`.
- **Auth:** Bearer token — `Authorization: Bearer {api_key}`.
- **Pagination:** limit/offset — GET in query params, POST-query in JSON body; advances by page_size, stops on short page. Default 500 (notes capped at 50).
- **Streams:** Static registry gated by `streams_to_discover`. **Deep 3-level parent→child fan-out:** `objects→records/record_values/attributes`, `lists→entries/entry_values/attributes`, and grandchildren `attributes→options/statuses`, each enumerated per parent id.
- **Incremental:** `CreatedAtIncrementalMixin` — key `created_at`, POST body `filter:{created_at:{$gte:&lt;bookmark&gt;}}` + `sorts:created_at asc`; applied to record/entries value streams only; catalog/attribute streams FULL_TABLE.
- **Record shaping:** flattens nested `id` object onto row; JSON-stringifies some fields; **child-explode** of `values`/`entry_values` maps into one row per (record, attribute_slug, value_index) with parent-id injection + computed `value_index`; parents strip the value maps.
- **Parity risk: HIGH** — POST-body query endpoints with in-body limit/offset+filter/sorts (non-standard); deep 3-level fan-out with per-object/list enumeration; per-attribute grandchild enumeration gated on type; same-endpoint value-map child-explode; offset paging is not order-stable.

## tap-campfire
Files: `base.py`, `streams.py`, `tap.py`, `schemas/`
- **Source/category:** Campfire (meetcampfire.com) — AI accounting / finance ERP.
- **Mechanism (EXACT): REST-JSON.** Base `https://api.meetcampfire.com`; envelope `$.results[*]` (child/list endpoints `$[*]`); paths e.g. `/coa/api/v1/invoice`, `/rr/api/v1/contracts`, `/coa/api/journal_entry`.
- **Auth:** api-key in header `Authorization: Token {api_key}` (**"Token " scheme, not Bearer**).
- **Pagination:** offset/limit — `CampfireOffsetPaginator` page_size=1000, stops on short page; `SinglePagePaginator` for budget_accounts &amp; subscriptions.
- **Streams:** 13 static classes, filtered by `streams_to_discover`. Parent→child fan-out via `partitions`: subscriptions per contract_id, budget_accounts per budget_id, chart_accounts per statement_type, chart_transactions per monthly window.
- **Incremental: NONE** — `replication_key=None` on every stream (full-refresh each run). `chart_transactions`/`journal_entries` take `start_date` as a request-window filter, not Singer state. Note: local meltano.yml shows `upsert:true`+`dedupe_before_upsert:true` (keyed upsert on id), so destination is not truncate-replace despite the "overwrite" hint.
- **Record shaping:** nested objects/arrays + dates JSON-stringified; `flatten_keys`; credit_memos casts 4 decimal-string amounts to float; budget_accounts injects parent `budget_id`; **contracts merges runtime-discovered `custom_field_*` columns from a 100-record live sample** (tenant-variable column set).
- **Parity risk:** offset paging with fixed 1000 page_size (no cursor; monthly partitioning bounds offsets); full-refresh source + target-side upsert/dedupe on `id` → model as `write_mode: upsert` keyed on id (append would duplicate); "Token " auth prefix; N+1 fan-out joins; runtime schema discovery; `journal_entries` needs explicit `start_date`/`end_date=9999-12-31` or Campfire silently drops rows to a trailing-6-month default.

# PART A — Meltano taps

Both taps are thin `singer-sdk` REST taps built on a shared `DriveRestStream` base from `github.com/DrivetrainAi/meltano-utils@v0` (NOT cloned — see parity risk). Both: Python 3.10–3.12, opt-in stream selection via `streams_to_discover` (comma-separated; **empty = sync nothing**), `start_date` (RFC 3339, default floor 2024-01-01).

## tap-anthropic
Files: `g8/anthropic/tap_anthropic/{base,streams,constants,tap}.py`, `schemas/*.py`

- **Source system**: Anthropic **Admin API**, `https://api.anthropic.com` (`constants.py:API_BASE_URL`; `ANTHROPIC_VERSION = "2023-06-01"`).
- **Auth**: static `x-api-key: &lt;admin key&gt;` + `anthropic-version` header (`base.py:89` `http_headers`). No OAuth/refresh. Requires an admin key (`sk-ant-admin01-…`).
- **Pagination** (`base.py`): two styles, **fail-loud** (raises if `has_more=true` but cursor missing — never silently truncates).
  - `AnthropicCursorPaginator`: list endpoints, `after_id` param + `has_more`/`last_id` (`limit=500`).
  - `AnthropicPageTokenPaginator`: report endpoints, opaque `next_page` token → `page` param.
- **Streams** (`streams.py:ALL_STREAMS`):
  - `api_keys` `/v1/organizations/api_keys` (PK `id`; flattens `created_by`)
  - `users` `/v1/organizations/users` (PK `id`)
  - `workspaces` `/v1/organizations/workspaces` (PK `id`; `include_archived=true`; flattens `data_residency`; `tags`→string)
  - `messages_usage` `/v1/organizations/usage_report/messages` — daily buckets, `group_by[]`=api_key_id,workspace_id,model,service_tier,context_window; flattens `cache_creation`,`server_tool_use`
  - `cost_report` `/v1/organizations/cost_report` — group_by workspace_id,description; `amount`→`amount_raw`+`amount_usd` (raw/100)
  - `api_key_costs` — derived: re-hits `usage_report/messages`, applies bundled `pricing.py` per (date,api_key,model), joins api-key names
  - `claude_code_usage` `/v1/organizations/usage_report/claude_code` — **one HTTP call per calendar day** (`YYYY-MM-DD`, no pagination), explodes nested `model_breakdown`; floor 2025-02-01
- **Incremental**: report streams are `replication_key = "starting_at"`, `is_sorted`. Report endpoints capped at **31-day windows** (`USAGE_REPORT_MAX_DAYS`), chunked client-side; end floored to UTC midnight (whole complete days only). Dim/list streams are full-refresh (`replication_key=None`).
- **Record shaping**: `parse_response` flattens bucket `{starting_at, ending_at, results:[…]}` → one self-contained row per result. `fill_key_nulls` replaces NULL in **string PK columns** with sentinel `"__unspecified__"` (`constants.py:KEY_NULL_SENTINEL`) so null-keyed rows stay upsert-able (BQ `NULL=NULL` is not TRUE). `flatten_keys`/`convert_to_string` handled in `DriveRestStream`.

## tap-openai
Files: `g8/openai/tap_openai/{base,streams,constants,tap}.py`

- **Source system**: OpenAI **Admin API**, `https://api.openai.com`, all under `/v1/organization/*` (requires `sk-admin-…`).
- **Auth**: static `Authorization: Bearer &lt;admin key&gt;` (`base.py:82`).
- **Pagination**: `OpenAICursorPaginator` = `after` param + `has_more`/`last_id` (`limit=100`), fail-loud. Report streams **deliberately avoid** the API `next_page` token (known OpenAI bug) and use `SinglePagePaginator` + client-side date windows instead.
- **Streams** (`streams.py:ALL_STREAMS`):
  - `projects` `/v1/organization/projects` (PK `id`; `include_archived=true`)
  - `users` `/v1/organization/users` (PK `id`)
  - `admin_api_keys` `/v1/organization/admin_api_keys` (PK `id`; flattens `owner`)
  - `project_api_keys` `/v1/organization/projects/{project_id}/api_keys` (PK `project_id,id`; flattens `owner`)
  - `project_users` `/v1/organization/projects/{project_id}/users` (PK `project_id,id`)
  - `completions_usage` `/v1/organization/usage/completions` — group_by project_id,user_id,api_key_id,model,batch,service_tier; bucket_limit **31**
  - `costs` `/v1/organization/costs` — group_by project_id,line_item; bucket_limit **180**; flattens nested `amount`→`amount_value`/`amount_currency`/`amount_usd`
- **Child streams**: `_ProjectChildStream` self-partitions over all projects internally (via `partitions`, NOT singer `parent_stream_type`) so each stream runs standalone in xiphos-V2 isolation; injects `project_id` onto each record in `post_process`.
- **Incremental**: report streams `replication_key = "start_time"` (unix epoch seconds), `is_sorted`; windows of `bucket_limit` days walked to `now`. Accepts either ISO bookmark (first run) or epoch (`_start_epoch`). List streams full-refresh.
- **Record shaping**: `timestamp_keys` — **unix epoch → RFC 3339 string** conversion in `post_process` (`base.py:96`) so BQ gets TIMESTAMP not INT. `object` discriminator dropped. `parse_response` flattens bucket envelope onto each result.

**Parity risk (both taps)**: (1) The nested-object flattening (`flatten_keys` → `parent_child` scalar columns) and `convert_to_string` are implemented in the **un-cloned `meltano-utils.DriveRestStream`** — the exact flatten naming/delimiter must be reproduced (schemas already declare the flat column names, e.g. `cache_creation_ephemeral_5m_input_tokens`, so the delimiter is `_` and format is `&lt;parentkey&gt;_&lt;childkey&gt;`). (2) The `"__unspecified__"` sentinel in string PKs (anthropic) and epoch→RFC3339 conversion (openai) are post-processing that a faucet REST source must replicate exactly or PKs/timestamps diverge. (3) `api_key_costs` (anthropic) applies bundled `pricing.py` math — reproducing that stream requires the same pricing table. (4) Report windowing differs (anthropic 31d client chunks; openai 31/180 day windows) — affects request shape but not table content.

# PART B — Sink: DrivetrainAi/target-bigquery (z3z1ma fork)

Effective config: `denormalized: true`, `method: batch_job`, `schema_resolver_version: "2"`, `column_name_transforms.add_underscore_when_invalid: true`, `cluster_on_key_properties: true`. Anthropic: `batch_size: 10000`, `upsert:[messages_usage,cost_report,api_key_costs,claude_code_usage]` + `overwrite:[api_keys,users,workspaces]` (disjoint). OpenAI: `batch_size: 10000`, `overwrite: true` (all streams). (The `batch_size: 5000` in the task prompt is not what the committed configs use.) Sink class resolved by `target.py:get_sink_class` → **`BigQueryBatchJobDenormalizedSink`** (`batch_job.py:137`).

## Table naming — `core.py:table_name` (lines 634–647)
From the **stream name**, unless `use_stream_name_as_table_name` is set (it is not here). Transform chain:
1. `stream.replace("-", "_").replace(".", "_")`
2. `camel_to_snake(name, seperate_letter_and_number_in_table_name=True)` (`core.py:1567`) — inserts `_` between camelCase boundaries **and between letters↔digits** (e.g. `foo2` → `foo_2`).
3. collapse repeated `_` → single, strip leading/trailing `_`, `.lower()`.

These stream names are already snake_case, so tables are essentially identical to the stream name: `api_keys`, `messages_usage`, `cost_report`, `claude_code_usage`, `completions_usage`, `costs`, `project_api_keys`, etc. **No dataset prefix/suffix** — dataset comes from the `dataset` config; table = derived name. **Parity gotcha**: the letter↔digit split (`separate_letter_and_number=True` default) — any stream name with an adjacent letter/digit would get an underscore inserted.

## Column mapping — `SchemaTranslator` (`core.py:1129`), `denormalized: true`
Denormalized = **one flat top-level BQ column per top-level JSON-schema property** (`Denormalized.preprocess_record` → `translate_record`, `core.py:960`; contrast fixed-schema mode which wraps everything in one `data` JSON column). Column names run through `transform_column_name`.

**`schema_resolver_version: "2"`** (`_jsonschema_property_to_bigquery_column`, V2 branch lines 1244–1294): far more lenient than V1 — **falls back to `JSON` for anything unresolvable** instead of erroring. Behavior:
- `anyOf` → uses index [0]’s type/format.
- **array**: no `items`/no item `type`/`patternProperties` → `JSON REPEATED`; item type record → `RECORD REPEATED`; else scalar `&lt;type&gt; REPEATED`.
- **object**: no `properties`, empty `properties`, or `patternProperties` present → **`JSON NULLABLE`** (whole object stored as a JSON value); otherwise recurse into a `RECORD` with nested fields (`_translate_record_to_bigquery_schema`, line 1298 — empty props → `JSON`).
- **scalar**: `patternProperties` → `JSON`; else `bigquery_type()`.
- On **any exception** → `JSON NULLABLE` (with a table/field-name special-case in `IGNORE_JSON_FIELDS_FOR_TABLES` → `STRING`).

So nested objects **with a declared schema** become native BQ `RECORD` (STRUCT); schemaless/dynamic objects and arrays-of-unknown become `JSON`; arrays of scalars/records become `REPEATED`. (For these taps most objects are pre-flattened/typed, so expect mostly scalar columns + a few RECORD/JSON.)

**Type map — `bigquery_type` (`core.py:1498`)**: `format:date-time`→`TIMESTAMP`; `date`→`DATE`; `time`→`TIME`; `number`→`FLOAT`; `integer`+`string`→`STRING`; `integer`→`INTEGER`; `boolean`→`BOOLEAN`; `object`→`RECORD`; **else `STRING`** (default/unknown). All scalar fields land `NULLABLE`.

**`column_name_transforms.add_underscore_when_invalid: true`** — `transform_column_name` (`core.py:1524`): strips backticks; removes Unicode whitespace/control/zero-width chars; **if first char is a digit, prepends `_`** (the "add_underscore_when_invalid" behavior); replaces `.`→`_` (`replace_period_with_underscore` is force-set True in translation, lines 1156/1167); and **always** replaces special chars `[!@#$%^&amp;*()/,]`→`_`. Note: `lower`/`snake_case` are **not** enabled by this config, so column casing is preserved as-is from the tap (only the invalid-char/leading-digit fixes apply). The function is `@cache`d.

## `_sdc_` metadata columns — `constants.py:DEFAULT_SCHEMA` / `SDC_FIELDS`; added by `preprocess_record` (`core.py:669`)
Every record gets exactly these 6 system columns popped out and attached (order as emitted). In denormalized mode they sit **alongside** the flat data columns (in fixed mode they accompany the single `data` JSON column):

| Column | BQ type |
|---|---|
| `_sdc_extracted_at` | TIMESTAMP |
| `_sdc_received_at` | TIMESTAMP |
| `_sdc_batched_at` | TIMESTAMP |
| `_sdc_deleted_at` | TIMESTAMP |
| `_sdc_sequence` | INT64 |
| `_sdc_table_version` | INTEGER |

No other system columns (no `_sdc_primary_key`, no `_sdc_received_at`-style extras beyond the above). **For byte-identical tables faucet-sink-bigquery must add these six columns, with these exact names/types, populated with the same semantics** (extracted_at from the tap's record `time_extracted`; received_at/batched_at = target-side timestamps; sequence = ms epoch ordering; deleted_at usually null; table_version usually null for these taps).

## Load method — `batch_job` (`batch_job.py`) + `load_with_retry` (`core.py:1051`)
- **Direct BigQuery load job, no GCS staging** (GCS is a separate `gcs_stage` method). Records are serialized to **newline-delimited JSON** (`orjson.dumps`, `process_record`), buffered and **gzip-compressed in memory** (`Compressor`), then loaded via `client.load_table_from_file(BytesIO(data), …)`.
- `LoadJobConfig` (denormalized, `batch_job.py:139`): `source_format = NEWLINE_DELIMITED_JSON`, `write_disposition = WRITE_APPEND`, `schema_update_options = [ALLOW_FIELD_ADDITION]`, `ignore_unknown_values = True`, explicit `schema = table.get_resolved_schema()`. One load job per batch (`batch_size`); parallelism via worker pool (`options.max_workers: 1` here → single worker).
- Retries transient job errors up to 3×.

**`overwrite` (WRITE_TRUNCATE full replace) — staging + swap:**
1. On init (`core.py:414`, `_is_overwrite_candidate` supports bool `true` OR fnmatch list), the real table is created (`create_target`), then `self.table` is repointed to a **staging table** `{name}__{YYYYMMDDHHMMSS}__{uuid}` (`_create_overwrite_table`, line 424; expires in `staging_table_expiration_days`, default 3).
2. All batches WRITE_APPEND into staging.
3. `clean_up` (`core.py:872`): a **`WRITE_TRUNCATE` query job** `SELECT * FROM staging` → destination = the real `overwrite_target` (atomic full replace; kept as a query job, not DDL, so it works on partitioned tables). If `key_properties` exist it **dedups**: `QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY &lt;keys&gt; ORDER BY _sdc_extracted_at DESC, _sdc_received_at DESC)=1`. Then drops staging. Optional `overwrite_min_row_ratio` shrink guard can refuse a suspiciously small snapshot.
4. State is **withheld** until the swap completes (`defers_state`, `core.py:742`) so an interrupted run never advances the bookmark past unwritten rows.

**`upsert` (MERGE on key_properties) — staging + MERGE** (`_is_upsert_candidate`, line 448; only when `key_properties` present AND denormalized):
- Staging table receives batches; `merge_table` (`core.py:765`) runs `MERGE target USING staging ON target.&lt;k&gt;=source.&lt;k&gt; (AND … per key) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET &lt;all matching cols&gt; WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (…)`, then drops staging. Runs in `pre_state_hook` (before each state msg) and `clean_up`. Optional `dedupe_before_upsert` wraps the source in the same QUALIFY dedup.
- **`upsert` wins over `overwrite`** if a stream matches both (`__init__` checks upsert first); a stream with no `key_properties` falls through (upsert ignored).

## Clustering / partitioning — `create_target` (`core.py:690`) + `default_table_options` (`core.py:264`)
- **`cluster_on_key_properties: true`** → `clustering_fields = tuple(key_properties[:4])` (first 4 PKs; BQ's max). Falls back to default `["_sdc_batched_at"]` when a stream has no key_properties.
- **Partitioning**: no `partition_granularity` is configured, so the **default applies: `TimePartitioning(type=MONTH, field="_sdc_batched_at")`** (from `default_table_options`, merged in `as_table`). So every table is **month-partitioned on `_sdc_batched_at`** and clustered on the first ≤4 key columns. (If `partition_granularity` were set it would still partition on `_sdc_batched_at`, only changing the grain.)
- Staging tables inherit the same defaults; the final overwrite/merge writes into the pre-created real table which already carries the intended partition/cluster spec.

**Parity checklist for faucet-sink-bigquery to produce identical tables:**
1. Table name = snake_cased/letter-digit-split stream name (see naming rules), in the configured dataset, no prefix.
2. Denormalized flat columns from top-level JSON-schema props, typed via the V2 resolver rules (JSON fallback for unresolvable; RECORD for typed nested objects; REPEATED for arrays; TIMESTAMP/DATE/TIME/FLOAT/INTEGER/BOOLEAN/STRING per `bigquery_type`), names transformed with leading-digit `_` prefix + special-char→`_` + `.`→`_` (no lowercasing).
3. Append the **six `_sdc_*` columns** with exact names/types/semantics above.
4. Month-partition on `_sdc_batched_at`; cluster on `key_properties[:4]` (or `_sdc_batched_at` if none).
5. Load as gzipped NDJSON via a load job (WRITE_APPEND), with `ignore_unknown_values` + `ALLOW_FIELD_ADDITION`.
6. For overwrite: stage → dedup-by-key (`_sdc_extracted_at`/`_sdc_received_at` DESC) → `WRITE_TRUNCATE` swap; for upsert: stage → `MERGE` on keys. Note upsert-wins-over-overwrite tie-break.

Files cited: `target_bigquery/constants.py` (`DEFAULT_SCHEMA`, `SDC_FIELDS`), `target_bigquery/core.py` (`BigQueryTable`, `BaseBigQuerySink.__init__/_create_overwrite_table/_is_upsert_candidate/_is_overwrite_candidate/table_name/preprocess_record/create_target/merge_table/clean_up`, `Denormalized`, `SchemaTranslator`, `bigquery_type`, `transform_column_name`, `camel_to_snake`), `target_bigquery/batch_job.py` (`BigQueryBatchJobDenormalizedSink.job_config/process_record/process_batch`, `load_with_retry`), `target_bigquery/target.py` (`get_sink_class`, upsert/overwrite config docs).

The `meltano_taps` clone was left untouched; all clones are under `scratchpad/g8/`.</result>

---

## sharepointsites (repo: DrivetrainAi/tap-sharepointsites, fork of storebrand/radbrt)

- **Source system / category:** Microsoft SharePoint document libraries — file-based ELT (spreadsheets/CSVs stored in SharePoint sites), finance/ops flat files.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** File-over-Microsoft-Graph. The `FilesStream` talks to `https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0`: resolves site→drive (`/sites/{host}:/{type}/{name}:` → `/sites/{id}/drive`), fetches file metadata by item-id (`/drives/{driveId}/items/{fileId}`), then **downloads the raw file bytes** via `@microsoft.graph.downloadUrl` (or `/items/{id}/content`) and parses them client-side. It does NOT use the Graph Excel/workbook API. **File formats downloaded+parsed: CSV** (streamed line-by-line into `csv.DictReader`, `csv_handler.py`) and **Excel .xlsx/.xls** (downloaded whole, parsed with **openpyxl** `read_only`, `excel_handler.py`). JSON is not a parsed format here. `client.py` also supports `lists` (Graph SharePoint list REST, `$.value[*]`) and `pages` (Graph beta pages), but the meltano config only drives `files`. Cite: `client.py`, `file_stream.py`, `file_handlers/{csv,excel}_handler.py`.
- **Auth:** `mode: sharepoint` → **Azure AD app-only** via `azure.identity.ClientSecretCredential(tenant_id, client_id, client_secret)` requesting scope `https://graph.microsoft.com/.default`; wrapped in `BearerTokenAuthenticator`. Least-privilege `Sites.Selected` model (per-site admin grant). Cite: `client.py::authenticator`.
- **Pagination:** `GraphHATEOASPaginator` follows `@odata.nextLink` for list/folder responses; individual file downloads are single-shot streamed GETs (Range-limited for schema discovery, 5 MB prefix). Cite: `client.py`, `file_stream.py`.
- **Streams:** Dynamic, **one Singer stream per file** enumerated from an **S3 config JSON** (`{"tables":[...]}`) by `meltano_taps/sharepointsites/pre.py`, which rewrites `meltano.yml`'s `files:` list. Each entry carries `file_pattern`(=`idAtSource` item id), `file_type`, `site_url`(=env `TAP_SHAREPOINTSITES_API_URL`, one URL for all files), `sheet_name`/`sheet_id`, and optional `dataFormatType`. Excel with a `sheet_id` becomes stream `name__{sheet_id}`. No parent→child fan-out. Cite: `pre.py`, `tap.py::discover_streams`.
- **Incremental/replication:** Effectively **full-refresh** (target `overwrite: true`). The tap has a soft filter — `parse_response` skips files whose `lastModifiedDateTime &lt;= starting replication value` (defaults `1900-01-01`) — but no replication_key is emitted; each run re-downloads and re-parses.
- **Record shaping in the tap:** Heavy. CSV→DictReader rows; Excel→openpyxl with **column-type inference** (scans rows → integer/number/boolean/date/date-time/string) and JSON-Schema build; column names normalized (`normalize_name`, optional `snakecase`/`clean_colnames`); `#REF!`/error cells blanked to null; empty-row cutoff (1000); `_loaded_at` stamped. **`data_format_type: timeseries` (Excel only) UNPIVOTS** a wide period-header sheet into long `(dimensions…, Month=date, Value=number)` records — a genuine reshape/denormalize done inside the tap. Cite: `excel_handler.py::_timeseries_rows`, `file_stream.py::stream_schema/parse_response`.
- **Parity risk:** **High.** Requires Graph file download + **openpyxl Excel parsing** with per-column type inference, the **timeseries unpivot transform**, CSV streaming, S3-driven dynamic stream generation, and Range-based bounded schema discovery. A Rust reimplementation must reproduce the Excel semantics (openpyxl date typing, error-cell blanking, unpivot math) exactly to match downstream tables.

## onedrive (SAME repo: tap-sharepointsites; config differs)

- **Source system / category:** Microsoft OneDrive (personal/business) — same file-based ELT tap, different Graph drive + auth.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** Identical code path (`FilesStream` over Graph `v1.0`), but drive resolution branches on hostname: for `onedrive.live.com`/`*-my.sharepoint.com` it uses **`/me/drive`** and `/me/drive/items/{id}` (+ name/search fallback `/me/drive/root/search(q=…)`), downloading + parsing the same **CSV / Excel(openpyxl)** formats. Cite: `file_stream.py::get_drive_id/_resolve_download_url/_search_file_in_onedrive`.
- **How the two meltano configs differ (key ask):**
  - `sharepointsites/meltano.yml`: `mode: sharepoint`, config = `client_secret` + `tenant_id` (+ `client_id`, `api_url` via env) → **ClientSecretCredential app-only auth**. `pre.py` sets each file's `site_url` from a **single env var** `TAP_SHAREPOINTSITES_API_URL` and **does** propagate `dataFormatType` (timeseries).
  - `onedrive/meltano.yml`: `mode: onedrive`, config = `refresh_token` + `client_secret` (+ `client_id`) → **refresh-token OAuth2** (`POST https://login.microsoftonline.com/common/oauth2/v2.0/token`, `grant_type=refresh_token`, scope `.default offline_access`; MS rotates the refresh token). `pre.py` sets each file's `site_url` from a **per-file `file_info["url"]`** and in practice omits `dataFormatType` (ENG-84395). No `tenant_id`/`api_url`. Both otherwise share `pre.py` logic, target-bigquery `overwrite`/`denormalized` config, and postgres state backend. Cite: `client.py::authenticator` (both branches), both `pre.py`, both `meltano.yml`.
- **Auth:** delegated **OAuth2 refresh-token** flow against the `common` tenant (see above). A companion `_post_refresh_token_helper` pattern persists rotated tokens (used by deskera too).
- **Pagination / Streams / Incremental / Shaping / Parity:** same as sharepointsites (dynamic per-file streams from S3 config, full-refresh overwrite, CSV/Excel parse + optional timeseries unpivot). Parity risk **High**, same reasons.

## afas (DrivetrainAi/tap-afas)

- **Source system / category:** AFAS Profit / AFAS Online (Dutch ERP/HRM) — ERP REST source.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **REST-JSON** over ProfitRestServices. Base `https://{environment_id}.rest.afas.online/ProfitRestServices` (or `base_url` on-prem). Records from `GET /connectors/{connector}` under `$.rows[*]`. Cite: `base.py`, `streams.py`.
- **Auth:** Custom header **`Authorization: AfasToken &lt;base64(token-XML)&gt;`** built from a config `token` (accepts full XML, its base64, or hex `data`). Cite: `auth.py::build_afas_auth_header`.
- **Pagination:** `skip`/`take` query params; advance `skip` by `take` until a short page (`AfasSkipTakePaginator`; AFAS gives no reliable total). Cite: `base.py`.
- **Streams:** **Dynamically discovered** — `GET /metainfo` lists every GetConnector → one stream each (or restricted via `connectors` config), name-deduped. No parent→child. Cite: `tap.py::discover_streams`, `streams.py::discover_all_connectors`.
- **Incremental/replication:** **Full refresh only** (`replication_key = None`; target `overwrite: true`).
- **Record shaping:** Schema per stream from `GET /metainfo/get/{connector}` field defs (AFAS datatype→JSON type), else sampled from one row, else passthrough `_raw`. `post_process` JSON-stringifies nested dict/list, Decimal→float, and normalizes date fields against the schema. Cite: `streams.py::fetch_connector_schema`, `base.py::post_process`.
- **Parity risk:** **Low.** Plain paginated REST-JSON; the only nuances are the AfasToken header, metainfo-driven dynamic streams, and X-Profit-Error decoding.

## acumatica (DrivetrainAi/tap-acumatica)

- **Source system / category:** Acumatica Cloud ERP — ERP REST source (OData-flavored contract-based endpoints).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **REST-JSON with OData query semantics.** Base `{instance_url}/entity/Default/{version}`; each entity is a path (`/Account`, `/Bill`, …). Uses **OData params** `$top`, `$skip`, `$orderby`, `$filter` (`ge datetimeoffset'…' and … lt …`), and **`$expand`** for nested detail (Details, Contacts/Address, etc.). Records from top-level JSON array `$[*]`; every scalar is `{"value":X}`-wrapped and unwrapped. Cite: `base.py`, `streams.py`.
- **Auth:** **Cookie-session login** (not OAuth): `POST /entity/auth/login {name,password[,company,branch,locale]}` → `Set-Cookie`, sent as `Cookie` header; `POST /entity/auth/logout` at exit; auto **re-login on HTTP 401** (`RetriableAPIError`). Cite: `auth.py::AcumaticaSession`.
- **Pagination:** Custom **`WindowedReplicationPaginator`** — tiles `[bookmark..now]` into **calendar-month windows**, `$skip`-pages within each window, walks empty leading windows. Keyless (static) streams fall back to plain `OffsetPaginator`. This exists to defeat Acumatica's non-strict `$orderby` skip-gap record loss (ENG-77809). Cite: `base.py`.
- **Streams:** **Static, curated** set of 11 (`streams_to_discover` selects; `*`=all): account, bill, currency, customer, financial_period, invoice, journal_transaction, journal_transaction_detail, payment, stock_item, vendor. **Parent→child fan-out:** `journal_transaction_detail` is a singer-sdk child of `journal_transaction`, emitted from the parent's `$expand=Details` via an in-process `_details_cache` keyed by `(BatchNbr, Module)` — **no extra API call**. Cite: `streams.py`.
- **Incremental/replication:** Document streams (bill/customer/invoice/journal_transaction/payment/vendor) are **incremental** on `LastModifiedDateTime` (singer-sdk max-bookmark, non-strict-sort tolerant; target `upsert: true` + `dedupe_before_upsert`). Static dimensions (account, currency, financial_period, stock_item[`LastModified`]) are **full-table, no replication_key** (deliberate, ENG-79419). Cite: `streams.py` docstrings, `meltano.yml`.
- **Record shaping:** `unwrap_value` on `{value:…}` wrapping; nested arrays/objects JSON-stringified (schema-aware: empty dict/list→null, non-string-typed nested→null to survive BigQuery load); child detail rows re-stamped with BatchNbr/Module. Cite: `base.py::post_process`, `streams.py`.
- **Parity risk:** **Medium-High.** OData `$filter/$orderby/$skip/$expand`, the **month-window paginator** (correctness-critical), cookie-session auth with 401 re-login, and the parent-cache child stream all need faithful reproduction; the `{value:…}` unwrap + nested-null coercion is load-bearing for downstream schema.

## deskera (DrivetrainAi/tap-deskera)

- **Source system / category:** Deskera Books (SMB accounting) — accounting REST source.
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **REST-JSON.** Base `https://api.deskera.com/v1` (`api_base_url`). Two shapes: **list endpoints** (`/account`, `/account/group`, `/contacts`, `/dimension`, `/journal`) and **date-windowed report endpoints** (`/account/reports/gl|trial-balance|pnl|balance-sheet|cash-flow`). Cite: `base.py`, `streams.py`, `reports.py`.
- **Auth:** **Deskera OAuth2 V2 refresh-token.** `DeskeraTokenManager` does `POST {token_url}?grant_type=refresh_token&amp;refresh_token=…` with **Basic** `client_id:client_secret`; access token sent as **`x-access-token`** header. Rotated refresh token written to `/tmp/refresh_token.txt` and persisted by `deskera/post.py` (`_post_refresh_token_helper.update_refresh_token` → drive API). Cite: `auth.py`, `post.py`.
- **Pagination:** List streams: **page-number** paginator (`page`/`limit`, stop on short page, `MAX_PAGES` guard). Report streams: `SinglePagePaginator` per window. Cite: `base.py::DeskeraPageNumberPaginator`, `reports.py`.
- **Streams:** **Static** set of 8 (`streams_to_discover`, empty=all). **Parent→child fan-out:** `account_subgroups` partitions **one request per account-group `code`** (fetched from `account_groups`) via `/account/group/subgroup/{group_code}`. Cite: `streams.py`.
- **Incremental/replication:** **Full refresh** (`replication_key = None`; target `overwrite`). Report streams iterate **month (or day) date windows** from `start_date`→now (`windows.py`) as a fetch strategy, not a bookmark. Cite: `reports.py::request_records`, `windows.py`.
- **Record shaping:** `extract_records` unwraps list envelopes (`LIST_RECORDS_KEYS`). **`journal` is denormalized** — header fields merged onto each line item (`_journal_lines` across several possible line-array keys), `customField` JSON-stringified. **Report streams do NOT normalize** — raw payload preserved wholesale as `report_json` string + window metadata (dbt normalizes downstream). Cite: `streams.py::JournalStream.parse_response`, `reports.py::parse_response`.
- **Parity risk:** **Medium.** OAuth refresh + token rotation persistence, per-group child partitioning, the journal header/line denormalize, and the date-windowed report fetch loop (with `report_json` passthrough) all need replicating; report schemas are provisional.

## dualentry (DrivetrainAi/tap-dualentry)

- **Source system / category:** DualEntry (modern AI accounting/ERP) — accounting REST source (API v2).
- **Mechanism (EXACT):** **REST-JSON.** Base = `API_BASE_URL` (`/public/v2/...`). Standard list endpoints return `{"items":[...],"count":N}` (records `$.items[*]`). Cite: `base.py`, `streams.py`.
- **Auth:** **API key** in header **`X-API-Key`** (`APIKeyAuthenticator`); the key itself is obtained out-of-band via DualEntry's `POST /public/v2/oauth/token` exchange. Cite: `base.py::authenticator`, README.
- **Pagination:** **Offset/limit** (`DualEntryOffsetPaginator`: `offset += limit`, `limit` capped 100, stop on short page). Cite: `base.py`.
- **Streams:** **Static, ~43 streams**, one per top-level list endpoint (schemas hand-defined under `schemas/`; `streams_to_discover` opt-in). **Parent→child fan-out (two patterns, no singer parent_stream_type — each stream self-contained for xiphos-V2 one-task-per-stream):**
  - `DualEntryLineItemStream`: lists parents, then issues **one `{path}/{number}/lines/` request per parent record**, stamping parent ids onto each line (`parent_key_map`), normalizing `line_type` item/expense→items/expenses. (Reflects July-2026 API change: lines no longer embedded.)
  - `DualEntryChildStream`: re-lists parent endpoint(s), takes the **cartesian product** across path levels (e.g. fixed-asset × depreciation-book), fetches parent-scoped child paths returning **bare arrays** (`$[*]`), stamps parent ids. Cite: `base.py::DualEntryLineItemStream/DualEntryChildStream`.
- **Incremental/replication:** **Full refresh** (no `replication_key`; `start_date` reserved for future incremental; target `overwrite: true` + `cluster_on_key_properties`). Cite: `base.py`, README, `meltano.yml`.
- **Record shaping:** `convert_to_string` JSON-stringifies flagged nested fields; line/child rows merged `{**line, **parent_fields}` (parent ids win, feed composite PKs); custom retry/backoff (`_get_json`/`_list_all`) for the manual sub-requests. Cite: `base.py`.
- **Parity risk:** **Medium.** Core listing is trivial offset REST, but the **per-parent line-item fan-out** (N+1 requests, parent-id stamping into composite keys) and the **cartesian-product child streams** are the reimplementation hazards; get the key-stamping wrong and dedupe/upsert silently collapses lines.

---

**Cross-cutting notes:** All six load to `target-bigquery` (z3z1ma fork) with `denormalized` + `schema_resolver_version:"2"`, postgres state backend. acumatica uses `upsert`+`dedupe_before_upsert`; the other five use `overwrite` (full-refresh). afas/acumatica/deskera/dualentry all build on the shared `DrivetrainAi/meltano-utils` (singer-sdk wrapper, logging, retry, token-file persistence). The sharepointsites/onedrive clone was left intact; clones are under `.../scratchpad/g6/`.</result>

# Incremental-Replication Machinery Map — faucet-stream

## Key distinction up front
There are **two different "replication" concepts** in the tree, and your #513 push-down belongs to neither of the existing ones — it's a new third thing that binds a stored bookmark into the outgoing request:
- **Per-source incremental** (client-side filtering): `crates/core/src/replication.rs` + REST `config.rs`/`stream.rs`. This is what you extend.
- **Snapshot→CDC orchestrator** (`replication:` top-level YAML block): `cli/src/replication/*` + `PipelineConfig.replication`. Unrelated to push-down; do not confuse the two.

---

## 1. `crates/core/src/replication.rs` (280 lines)

**`ReplicationMethod`** — `replication.rs:9-18`
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, JsonSchema)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum ReplicationMethod { #[default] FullTable, Incremental }
```

**`filter_incremental`** — `replication.rs:30`
```rust
pub fn filter_incremental(records: Vec&lt;Value&gt;, key: &amp;str, start: &amp;Value) -&gt; Vec&lt;Value&gt;
```
Behavior: keeps `record[key] &gt; start` (strict). Missing key → excluded. **Type mismatch** between key value and `start` → record is **kept** with a `tracing::warn!` (deliberate anti-data-loss, #78/#27). Uses `json_gt`.

**`max_replication_value`** — `replication.rs:49`
```rust
pub fn max_replication_value&lt;'a&gt;(records: &amp;'a [Value], key: &amp;str) -&gt; Option&lt;&amp;'a Value&gt;
```
Max of `record[key]` across records via `json_compare`.

**`max_value`** — `replication.rs:59` — `pub fn max_value(a: Value, b: Value) -&gt; Value` (folds running max across pages).

**`json_gt`** — `replication.rs:140` — `pub fn json_gt(a: &amp;Value, b: &amp;Value) -&gt; bool` (== `json_compare(a,b)==Greater`).

**Supporting (private):** `type_rank` (`:68`), `number_as_i128` (`:82`, i128 to avoid f64 precision loss above 2^53), `json_compare` (`:96`, `pub(crate)`). Ordering: integers exact as i128, floats as f64 (NaN last), strings lexicographic (so RFC3339 orders correctly), different types by `type_rank`.

**There are NO config structs here** — the config lives per-source (see §4). Core only provides the comparison/filter primitives.

---

## 2. Bookmark read/apply — `crates/core/src/traits.rs` (Source trait)

- **`state_key`** — `traits.rs:169` — `fn state_key(&amp;self) -&gt; Option&lt;String&gt;` (default `None`). Opt-in key for `StateStore` persistence.
- **`apply_start_bookmark`** — `traits.rs:181` — `async fn apply_start_bookmark(&amp;self, _bookmark: Value) -&gt; Result&lt;(), FaucetError&gt;` (default `Ok(())`). Sources store behind interior mutability, consult inside `fetch_with_context_incremental`.
- **`capture_resume_position`** — `traits.rs:196` — `async fn capture_resume_position(&amp;self) -&gt; Result&lt;Option&lt;Value&gt;, FaucetError&gt;` (default `Ok(None)`). Used by snapshot→CDC orchestrator, not incremental push-down.
- Related: `fetch_with_context_incremental` (`:36`), `fetch_all_incremental` (`:45`), default `stream_pages` attaches bookmark to final page only (`:77`, logic at `:88-112`).

**`StreamPage`** — `crates/core/src/pipeline.rs:96-101`
```rust
pub struct StreamPage { pub records: Vec&lt;Value&gt;, pub bookmark: Option&lt;Value&gt; }
```
(also `PipelineResult.bookmark` at `pipeline.rs:115`.)

**Pipeline persistence** (`pipeline.rs`): `with_state_store` (`:185`). In `run` (~`:389-490`): reads prior state, `unwrap_state` splits `(bookmark, seq)` (`:395`), calls `apply_start_bookmark` (`:398`/`:401`/`:425`) **before** fetch; then for every page carrying `Some(bookmark)` it flushes the sink and writes the bookmark **after** durable write (ADR 0002 checkpoint ordering, note at `:642`). Bookmark-lag gauges updated via `observability/bookmark.rs`.

---

## 3. CLI config grammar — `cli/src/config.rs` (2189 lines)

- **`ConnectorSpec`** — `config.rs:373-417`. Fields: `kind`(`type`), `config: Value`, `transforms`, `inherit_transforms`, `status: Option&lt;SourceStatus&gt;`, `tags: Vec&lt;String&gt;`, **`complete_for: Option&lt;CompletenessClaim&gt;`** (`:416`). `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`. **This is the exact template** for adding a per-source `replication: Option&lt;ReplicationBind&gt;` field — a new `Option&lt;...&gt;` on `ConnectorSpec` with `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]`, meaningful only on source templates.

- **`CompletenessClaim`** — `config.rs:424-439`: `{ scope: BTreeMap&lt;String,Value&gt;, on_missing: OnMissing }`. Note scope values "may carry `${parent.path}`/`${now.*}` tokens, resolved per invocation" — same tokening you'd want for `${bookmark}`. **`OnMissing`** enum (`:445`, snake_case, `Ignore`/`Delete`).

- **There is NO existing per-source `replication:` sub-block on `ConnectorSpec`.** The `config: Value` is passed verbatim to the connector's own `Deserialize` (so today REST's `replication_method`/`replication_key`/`start_replication_value`/`state_key` are set inside that opaque `config` blob). A new `bind:` sub-config would live either (a) as a new typed field on `ConnectorSpec` (mirroring `complete_for`), or (b) inside the connector's own config struct (§4). Given #513 is server-side/declarative and cross-connector, `ConnectorSpec.replication.bind` (option a) matches the `complete_for` precedent best.

- **`PartialConnector`** (matrix override) — `config.rs:460-478`: `{ r#ref: Option&lt;String&gt;, kind(type): Option&lt;String&gt;, config: Option&lt;Value&gt;, status: Option&lt;SourceStatus&gt; }`. `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`. Note it does **not** currently carry `complete_for` — if you want per-row override of the bind you must add a field here too (deep-merge semantics).

- **`PipelineConfig.replication`** — `config.rs:137` — `Option&lt;crate::replication::spec::ReplicationSpec&gt;` — this is the **snapshot→CDC** block (`cli/src/replication/spec.rs:19`, `mode: snapshot_then_cdc`, `snapshot`, `cdc`), NOT push-down. Test `parses_replication_block` at `config.rs:1404`.

- **Deep-merge / expand**: matrix rows deep-merge onto base (`config.rs:1108+`, `bind_document` at `:1174`). `${env:X}` then `${param.*}` binding happen pre-parse.

---

## 4. How the REST source applies replication today

**Config** — `crates/source/rest/src/config.rs`:
- imports `faucet_core::ReplicationMethod` (`:6`)
- `replication_method: ReplicationMethod` (`:92`), `replication_key: Option&lt;String&gt;` (`:94`), `start_replication_value: Option&lt;Value&gt;` (`:97`), `state_key: Option&lt;String&gt;` (`:104`). Defaults at `:184-187` (FullTable / None). Builder methods `:322-345`.
- Request-shaping fields (where a bind would inject): `base_url:String`(`:43`), `path:String` (with `{key}` placeholders, `:46`), `method`, `headers: HeaderMap`(`:54`), `query_params: HashMap&lt;String,String&gt;`(`:55`), `body: Option&lt;Value&gt;`(`:56`), `records_path`(`:60`). These are the four bind targets (query|header|body|path).

**Runtime** — `crates/source/rest/src/stream.rs`:
- imports `filter_incremental, max_replication_value, max_value, ReplicationMethod` (`:11-12`)
- `runtime_start: Mutex&lt;Option&lt;Value&gt;&gt;` holds the applied bookmark; resolved at `stream_pages_inner` top: `runtime_start` **takes precedence over** `config.start_replication_value` (`:383-388`).
- **client-side filter** in the page loop: `filter_incremental(raw_records, key, start)` when method==Incremental AND key+start both set (`:470-481`).
- running-max tracking across pages `:485-493`; bookmark attached to final page only (`:507-524`); trailing empty-page checkpoint `:539-544`.
- `Source::state_key` → `self.config.state_key.clone()` (`stream.rs:1051`).
- `Source::apply_start_bookmark` → `*self.runtime_start.lock().await = Some(bookmark)` (`stream.rs:1066-1069`).
- Request construction: `execute_request` (`:607`), `execute_request_once` (`:674`); URL/path built via `faucet_core::util::substitute_context(&amp;self.config.path, ctx)` (`:687`), then `{base_url}/{path}`; query params cloned from `config.query_params` at `:424`. **Today nothing injects the bookmark into query/header/body/path** — that gap is exactly #513.

`crates/source/postgres-cdc/src/replication.rs` also exists but is CDC/WAL, not the incremental-key path.

---

## 5. Date/epoch/ISO helpers reusable in core

- **`crates/core/src/observability/bookmark.rs`**: `parse_rfc3339_to_unix_seconds(s: &amp;str) -&gt; Option&lt;f64&gt;` (`:57`) — **hand-rolled, no chrono dep**; strict RFC3339 → unix seconds. `days_from_ymd(year,month,day) -&gt; Option&lt;i64&gt;` (`:126`). `update_bookmark_lag(bookmark:&amp;Value, labels:&amp;Labels) -&gt; bool` (`:26`). These are the epoch↔iso primitives in core, but note they're currently `fn`/private-ish (module is `observability::bookmark`; `update_bookmark_lag`/`parse` — `parse` is private `fn`). For your `format: epoch|iso` converter you'd likely need a reverse (unix→RFC3339) which does NOT exist yet.
- **`chrono` IS available and used** in `crates/core/src/transform.rs:1899-1900`: `chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(s)` → `.to_rfc3339_opts(chrono::SecondsFormat::AutoSi, true)`, and `:3015` normalizes `+00:00`→`Z`. So `chrono` (with rfc3339 + `DateTime::from_timestamp`) is a dependency you can reuse for epoch↔iso conversion rather than the hand-rolled parser.

---

## 6. Templating helpers — `crates/core/src/util.rs` (896 lines)

Placeholders use **`{key}`** syntax (single braces), not `${...}`. Note your spec's `${bookmark}` differs — you'll either adapt to `{bookmark}` or add a `${}` scanner.

- **`substitute_context`** — `util.rs:97` — `pub fn substitute_context(template: &amp;str, context: &amp;HashMap&lt;String, Value&gt;) -&gt; String`. Single-pass, unmatched `{key}` left verbatim, replacement never re-scanned. **This is the primary helper to render `gte|{bookmark}`** into a query/header/path string.
- **`substitute_context_bind_params`** — `util.rs:164` — `pub fn substitute_context_bind_params(template: &amp;str, context: &amp;HashMap&lt;String,Value&gt;, start_index: usize, placeholder: impl Fn(usize)-&gt;String) -&gt; (String, Vec&lt;Value&gt;)`. For SQL bind params ($1, $2…). Relevant if a future SQL-source push-down needs parameterized injection.
- **`substitute_context_json`** — `util.rs:215` — `pub fn substitute_context_json(template: &amp;str, context: &amp;HashMap&lt;String,Value&gt;) -&gt; String`. JSON-string-escaping variant — **use this for injecting into a JSON request `body`**.
- Shared engine `substitute_single_pass` (`:111`, private). Also `extract_records` (`:33`), `extract_context` (`:367`), `quote_ident` (`:21`), `redact_uri_credentials` (`:255`).
- `advance_from` JSONPath: reuse `extract_records`/JSONPath machinery — REST already extracts via `records_path` JSONPath in `crates/source/rest/src/extract.rs` (`extract::extract_records`, called `stream.rs:467`); that's the pattern for pulling `advance_from` out of a response body.

---

## Design pointers for #513
- Add `ReplicationBind` (declarative: `into: query|header|body|path`, `operator template` e.g. `"gte|${bookmark}"`, `format: epoch|iso`, `advance_from: &lt;JSONPath&gt;`) as `Option&lt;ReplicationBind&gt;` on **`ConnectorSpec`** (`cli/src/config.rs:417`), mirroring `complete_for` (`:416`) exactly (serde default + skip_if_none + source-only validation at expand time). Add matching field to **`PartialConnector`** (`:462`) if per-row override is needed.
- Wire it into REST at request build in `crates/source/rest/src/stream.rs` `execute_request_once` (`:674`) — inject rendered operator template into `params`/`headers`/`body`/`path` before the request, using `util::substitute_context`/`substitute_context_json` with a context map containing the resolved `bookmark` (from `runtime_start`/`effective_start`, resolved at `:383`).
- `advance_from` → pull next bookmark from the response body via the same JSONPath extraction used at `stream.rs:467` (`extract::extract_records`), as an alternative to the current `max_replication_value` client-side max.
- `format` epoch↔iso → reuse `chrono` (already a core dep, `transform.rs:1899`) rather than the private `parse_rfc3339_to_unix_seconds` in `observability/bookmark.rs`.

- **`advance(&amp;self, body: &amp;Value, headers: &amp;HeaderMap, state: &amp;mut PaginationState, record_count: usize) -&gt; Result&lt;bool, FaucetError&gt;`** — `:138-276`. - `body_cursor&lt;'a&gt;(&amp;'a self, state) -&gt; Option&lt;(&amp;'a str, &amp;'a str)&gt;` — `:283-293` (only `CursorInBody`; returns `(body_cursor_field, token)`).

### Per-style helpers (module-level functions)
- `cursor::apply_params(params, param_name, token: &amp;Option&lt;String&gt;)` — `cursor.rs:8-16`; `cursor::advance(body, next_token_path, next_token: &amp;mut Option&lt;String&gt;) -&gt; Result&lt;bool&gt;` — `cursor.rs:18-59` (reads scalar via `body.query(path)`; null/empty→stop; array/object→error).

- `offset::apply_params` / `offset::advance(body, offset, record_count, limit, total_path)` — `offset.rs:8-52`.

## 5. Incremental replication (current, client-side)

Model:
- Config fields: `replication_method` (`ReplicationMethod::{FullTable|Incremental}`, `crates/core/src/replication.rs:11-18`), `replication_key` (record field NAME, not JSONPath), `start_replication_value`, `state_key` — all in `config.rs`.
- Runtime bookmark override: `runtime_start` field on `RestStream` (`stream.rs:44`), set by `apply_start_bookmark` (`stream.rs:1066-1069`), read at `stream.rs:383-388` (takes precedence over `config.start_replication_value`).
- **Filtering (client-side push-down does NOT exist today):** `filter_incremental(raw_records, key, start)` at `stream.rs:475`, defined `crates/core/src/replication.rs:30-46` — keeps records where `record[key] &gt; start` (client-side, after fetch). There is currently NO server-side query-param binding of the bookmark into the request; the bookmark only filters already-downloaded records.
- **Max bookmark computation:** `max_replication_value(records, key)` (`replication.rs:49-54`) + `max_value(a,b)` (`replication.rs:59-64`) combine per-page maxima into `running_max` (`stream.rs:485-493`), attached to the final `StreamPage.bookmark` (`stream.rs:518-524`) or trailing empty page (`:539-544`).
- `StreamPage { records: Vec&lt;Value&gt;, bookmark: Option&lt;Value&gt; }` — `crates/core/src/pipeline.rs:96-102`.

This is the exact seam for your bookmark→request binding: `effective_start` (`stream.rs:383`) is available, and request params are built at `stream.rs:424-425` — a server-side push-down would inject `effective_start` into `params`/`body` there (e.g. OData `$filter=field gt &lt;bookmark&gt;`), rather than only feeding `filter_incremental`.

# Auth Machinery Map — faucet-stream

## 1. `crates/core/src/auth.rs` — the core abstraction

### `AuthProvider` trait (lines 103–144)
Object-safe (`Arc&lt;dyn AuthProvider&gt;`). `#[async_trait]`, supertraits `Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug`.

```rust
#[async_trait]
pub trait AuthProvider: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {
    // line 106 — required
    async fn credential(&amp;self) -&gt; Result&lt;Credential, FaucetError&gt;;

    // line 115 — defaulted (delegates to credential); CAS reauth hook
    async fn invalidate(&amp;self, _stale: &amp;Credential) -&gt; Result&lt;Credential, FaucetError&gt; {
        self.credential().await
    }

    // lines 133–140 — DEFAULTED, object-safe per-request signing hook (#496).
    // This is the "object-safe defaulted method already exists" the issue refers to.
    async fn sign_request(
        &amp;self,
        _method: &amp;str,
        _url: &amp;str,
        _query: &amp;std::collections::BTreeMap&lt;String, String&gt;,
    ) -&gt; Result&lt;Option&lt;Credential&gt;, FaucetError&gt; {
        Ok(None)
    }

    // line 143 — required
    fn provider_name(&amp;self) -&gt; &amp;'static str;
}
```

Key contract: `sign_request` returns `Ok(None)` by default; a signer overrides it to return `Some(Credential)` (typically `Credential::Header{ name:"Authorization", value }`). When `Some`, the connector uses it **instead of** `credential()` for that request. `query` is a `BTreeMap&lt;String,String&gt;` (ordered — matters for signature base strings). All args are borrowed primitives, keeping the trait object-safe. This is exactly the hook the new HMAC signer plugs into.

### `Credential` enum (lines 33–54) — **NOT `#[non_exhaustive]`** (deliberate; forces exhaustive mapping in connectors)
- `Bearer(String)`
- `Header { name: String, value: String }`
- `Basic { username: String, password: String }`
- `Token(String)` — raw, for connector-specific assembly
- Hand-written `Debug` redacts secrets (lines 62–79).
- `authorization_value(&amp;self) -&gt; Option&lt;String&gt;` (line 86): `Bearer`→`"Bearer {t}"`, `Token`→verbatim, `Header`/`Basic`→`None`.

### `SharedAuthProvider` (line 148)
```rust
pub type SharedAuthProvider = Arc&lt;dyn AuthProvider&gt;;
```

### `AuthReference` (lines 152–158)
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, JsonSchema)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct AuthReference {
    #[serde(rename = "ref")]
    pub name: String,
}
```

### `AuthSpec&lt;A&gt;` (lines 166–228) — models inline `{type,config}` vs `{ref}`
```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
#[serde(untagged)]
pub enum AuthSpec&lt;A&gt; {
    Inline(A),               // the { type, config } shape (A is the connector's Auth enum)
    Reference(AuthReference),// { ref: &lt;name&gt; }
}
```
- `Default for AuthSpec&lt;A: Default&gt;` → `Inline(A::default())` (line 175).
- `inline(&amp;self) -&gt; Option&lt;&amp;A&gt;` (line 183); `reference_name(&amp;self) -&gt; Option&lt;&amp;str&gt;` (line 191).
- **Manual `Deserialize`** (lines 201–228) enforces `ref`-XOR-inline: presence of `ref` + any other key is a hard error. This is the pattern the new "flow" provider's inline config `A` would slot into as `AuthSpec::Inline`.

**RequestSigner:** there is no separate `RequestSigner` trait — signing is the defaulted `AuthProvider::sign_request` method. No other signer hook exists in core.

**How a caller applies a credential today:** caller calls `provider.sign_request(method,url,query).await?`; if `None`, calls `provider.credential().await?`; then maps the returned `Credential` onto the wire protocol (see §4).

## 2. `crates/auth/` (faucet-auth crate)

### `lib.rs`
- Modules: `oauth1` (feature-gated `oauth1`), `oauth2`, `static_provider`, `token_endpoint` (lines 25–29).
- Re-exports (lines 51–55): `OAuth1Provider` (cfg oauth1), `OAuth2ClientCredentialsProvider`, `OAuth2RefreshProvider`, `StaticProvider`, `TokenEndpointProvider`.
- `auth_http_client() -&gt; reqwest::Client` (line 43) — 30s timeout; shared HTTP client builder. **Yes, faucet-auth already makes HTTP requests via reqwest** (json+form features).
- `DEFAULT_EXPIRY_RATIO: f64 = 0.9` (line 59).
- **`build_provider(spec: &amp;Value) -&gt; Result&lt;SharedAuthProvider, FaucetError&gt;`** (line 67) — the public entry. Reads `spec["type"]` (str) + `spec["config"]` (defaults `Null`). Dispatch (lines 74–98):
  - `"static"` → `StaticProvider::from_config`
  - `"oauth2"` → `OAuth2ClientCredentialsProvider::from_config`
  - `"oauth2_refresh"` → `OAuth2RefreshProvider::from_config`
  - `"token_endpoint"` → `TokenEndpointProvider::from_config`
  - `"oauth1"` → `OAuth1Provider::from_config` (behind `#[cfg(feature="oauth1")]`; else a Config error telling the user to rebuild with `--features oauth1`)
  - `other` → `FaucetError::Config` with the known-types list.
  - **To add the new flow provider you add one arm here (e.g. `"flow"`) plus a module.**
- `expiry_instant(expires_in: Option&lt;u64&gt;, expiry_ratio: f64) -&gt; Option&lt;tokio::time::Instant&gt;` (line 104).
- `parse_expiry_ratio(config: &amp;Value) -&gt; Result&lt;f64, FaucetError&gt;` (line 123) — validates `(0,1]`.

### `static_provider.rs` — `StaticProvider` (struct line 11, `{ credential: Credential }`)
- `bearer(impl Into&lt;String&gt;)`, `new(Credential)`, `from_config(&amp;Value)` (accepts `token` | `header`+`value` | `username`+`password`).
- `impl AuthProvider` (line 59): `credential()` clones the fixed cred; `provider_name()` = `"static"`. No refresh/sign override.

### `oauth2.rs` — two providers
`TokenResponse` (line 21): `access_token`, `expires_in: Option&lt;u64&gt;`, `refresh_token: Option&lt;String&gt;`, `token_type`.

**`OAuth2ClientCredentialsProvider`** (struct line 50): fields `http, token_url, client_id, client_secret, scopes: Vec&lt;String&gt;, expiry_ratio, state: Mutex&lt;CachedToken&gt;`.
- `from_config(&amp;Value)` (line 78). `fetch()` posts `grant_type=client_credentials` form (line 90).
- `impl AuthProvider` (line 106): `credential()` — single-flight (`self.state.lock().await` **held across the network fetch**); `invalidate(stale)` — CAS on `Bearer` token; `provider_name()`=`"oauth2"`.

**`OAuth2RefreshProvider`** (struct line 155): fields `http, token_url, client_id, client_secret, expiry_ratio, store: Option&lt;Arc&lt;dyn StateStore&gt;&gt;, store_key: String, state: Mutex&lt;RefreshState&gt;`. `RefreshState` (line 138): `access_token, expires_at, refresh_token: String, loaded: bool`.
- `from_config` (line 184); `with_store(self, store, key) -&gt; Self` (line 207).
- **Rotation/persistence (#499):** `ensure_loaded` (line 217, lazily reads persisted refresh_token once, non-fatal on error), `persist` (line 243, non-fatal write), `refresh` (line 252) — posts `grant_type=refresh_token`, captures rotated `refresh_token` in place (line 269) and persists it.
- `parse_persist(config, token_url, client_id) -&gt; Result&lt;(Option&lt;Arc&lt;dyn StateStore&gt;&gt;, String)&gt;` (line 281) — `persist.path` → `FileStateStore`, default key `oauth2_refresh_{fnv1a_64(token_url\0client_id):016x}`. `fnv1a_64` (line 316).
- `impl AuthProvider` (line 325): `credential()`/`invalidate()` single-flight + CAS; `provider_name()`=`"oauth2_refresh"`.
- Single-flight technique throughout: `Mutex&lt;...&gt;` locked across the fetch so concurrent callers await one in-flight request.

### `token_endpoint.rs` — `TokenEndpointProvider` (struct line 46) — **closest existing template for the new flow (JSONPath + placement)**
- Fields: `http, url, method: reqwest::Method, body: Option&lt;Value&gt;, encoding: BodyEncoding, token_path: String, expiry_path: Option&lt;String&gt;, expiry_ratio, apply_as: ApplyAs, state: Mutex&lt;CachedToken&gt;`.
- `enum ApplyAs { Bearer, Header { name, template } }` (line 26) — **credential placement**: `make_credential(token)` (line 128) renders `{token}` into a header template → `Credential::Header`, else `Credential::Bearer`. This is the current header/bearer placement model (no query/cookie/body-side placement yet).
- `enum BodyEncoding { Json, Form }` (line 34).
- `from_config` (line 79): `url`, `method` (default POST), `token_path` (required), `encoding`, `body`, `expiry_path`, `expiry_ratio`, `apply_as`.
- **JSONPath capture:** uses `jsonpath_rust::JsonPath` (`use ... JsonPath;` line 6). `extract_string(body, path)` (line 274) via `body.query(path)`; `extract_u64` (line 283). `parse_apply_as` (line 224); `form_pairs` (line 252).
- `impl AuthProvider` (line 172): `credential()` single-flight; `invalidate()` CAS comparing *rendered* credential (line 190); `provider_name()`=`"token_endpoint"`.

### `oauth1.rs` — `OAuth1Provider` — **the HMAC signer template**
- Feature-gated `#[cfg(feature="oauth1")]`. Deps: `hmac::{Hmac,Mac}`, `sha2::Sha256`, `base64::Engine`, `percent_encoding::{utf8_percent_encode, NON_ALPHANUMERIC, AsciiSet}`. `type HmacSha256 = Hmac&lt;Sha256&gt;` (line 19).
- `OAUTH_ENCODE` AsciiSet (line 24) + `enc(&amp;str)` (line 30) — RFC 3986 percent-encoding.
- Struct (line 35): `consumer_key, consumer_secret, token, token_secret, realm: Option&lt;String&gt;, nonce_counter: AtomicU64`. Hand-written `Debug` redacts secrets.
- `from_config` (line 64): requires `consumer_key/secret`, `token`, `token_secret`; optional `realm`; optional `signature_method` (only `HMAC-SHA256`).
- `impl AuthProvider` (line 94): `credential()` **returns `Err`** (no standalone credential); **`sign_request(method,url,query)`** (line 105) — builds timestamp + nonce (`{timestamp}{counter}` via AtomicU64), calls `authorization_header`, returns `Some(Credential::Header{name:"Authorization", value})`; `provider_name()`=`"oauth1"`.
- **HMAC signing core:** `authorization_header(method,url,query,nonce,timestamp) -&gt; String` (line 136): builds RFC 5849 base string (method &amp; base-url(query-stripped) &amp; sorted percent-encoded param string), signing key `enc(consumer_secret)&amp;enc(token_secret)`, `HmacSha256::new_from_slice(key).update(base).finalize()` → base64 STANDARD → assembles `OAuth realm=..., oauth_*=..., oauth_signature=...`. Pure/deterministic for fixed nonce+timestamp. **This is the exact HMAC pattern to reuse for the pluggable request signer.**

## 4. How rest/xml/graphql consume `AuthProvider` and apply per request

Each source stores `auth_provider: Option&lt;SharedAuthProvider&gt;` and exposes `with_auth_provider(mut self, provider: SharedAuthProvider) -&gt; Self`:
- REST: `crates/source/rest/src/stream.rs` field line 40, setter line 196.
- GraphQL: `crates/source/graphql/src/stream.rs` field line 27, setter line 173.
- XML: `crates/source/xml/src/stream.rs` field line 44, setter line 183.

**REST (fullest — the only source that calls `sign_request`)** `stream.rs`:
- `execute_request_once(...)` (line 674). Auth resolution lines 699–711:
  ```rust
  let resolved_auth = if let Some(provider) = &amp;self.auth_provider {
      let query: BTreeMap&lt;String,String&gt; = params.iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.clone(),v.clone())).collect();
      match provider.sign_request(self.config.method.as_str(), &amp;url, &amp;query).await? {
          Some(cred) =&gt; credential_to_auth(cred),
          None =&gt; credential_to_auth(provider.credential().await?),
      }
  } else { /* inline OAuth2 / TokenEndpoint via per-source caches, else AuthSpec::Reference → error */ };
  ```
- Then `resolved_auth.apply(&amp;mut headers)?` (line 772). `Auth::ApiKeyQuery` applied separately as a query param (line 794).
- `credential_to_auth(cred: Credential) -&gt; Auth` (line 126): `Bearer→Auth::Bearer`, `Token→Auth::Custom{Authorization}`, `Basic→Auth::Basic`, `Header→Auth::Custom{name:value}`.
- **Reauth-on-status today (F57):** `execute_request` wrapper (around line 620): on `Err(FaucetError::HttpStatus{status:401,..})` **and** `uses_inline_cached_token()` (line 647 — only when `auth_provider.is_none()` and inline OAuth2/TokenEndpoint), it calls `invalidate_inline_token_cache()` (line 658) and retries once. Note: shared-provider reauth via `AuthProvider::invalidate` is **not** currently wired at this 401 site (only inline caches are) — relevant for the new "reauth-on-status" feature.

**GraphQL** `stream.rs` line 299: `let effective_auth: GraphqlAuth = if let Some(provider) = &amp;self.auth_provider { credential_to_auth(provider.credential().await?) } else { ... }`. Does **not** call `sign_request`.

**XML** `stream.rs` line 395: `let effective_auth: XmlAuth = if let Some(provider) = &amp;self.auth_provider { credential_to_auth(provider.credential().await?) } else { ... }`. Does **not** call `sign_request`.

Both graphql/xml have their own `credential_to_auth` helpers and an `AuthSpec::Reference` → error path ("no provider was supplied; set one via the CLI `auth:` catalog or `with_auth_provider`").

### REST inline `Auth` enum — `crates/source/rest/src/auth/mod.rs`
```rust
#[serde(tag="type", content="config", rename_all="snake_case")]
pub enum Auth {  // line 20
    None, Bearer{token}, Basic{username,password},
    ApiKey{header,value}, ApiKeyQuery{param,value},
    OAuth2{token_url,client_id,client_secret,scopes,expiry_ratio},
    TokenEndpoint{url,method,headers,body,token_path,expiry_path,expiry_ratio,response_validator},
    Custom{headers: HashMap&lt;String,String&gt;},
}
```
`Auth::apply(&amp;self, headers: &amp;mut HeaderMap) -&gt; Result&lt;(),FaucetError&gt;` (line 100). Per-strategy `apply` fns in `auth/{api_key,basic,bearer,custom}.rs`. This is the connector-side `A` used as `AuthSpec&lt;Auth&gt;`. Note the existing placement surface here is header-only + one query param (`ApiKeyQuery`); no cookie/body placement exists yet.

## 5. Cargo deps available for the new provider

**Workspace `Cargo.toml`:** `async-trait 0.1.89` (223), `reqwest 0.13` +json/form/query (225), `jsonpath-rust 1.0.1` (227), `base64 0.22` (247), `sha2 0.10` (249), `hmac 0.12` (255), `percent-encoding 2` (331). **No `hex`** declared at workspace level (only transitive in Cargo.lock) — you'd add it if needed.

**`crates/auth/Cargo.toml`:** hard deps `faucet-core`, `async-trait`, `serde`(derive), `serde_json`, `tokio`(time,sync), **`reqwest`(json,form)** — so faucet-auth already does HTTP, `jsonpath-rust`, `tracing`. Optional (feature `oauth1`, lines 24–31): `hmac`, `sha2`, `base64`, `percent-encoding`. dev: `wiremock 0.6`, `futures`, `tempfile`.
→ For the new flow provider's HMAC signer + JSONPath capture + HTTP login chain, everything is already available; reuse the `oauth1` feature deps (or a new feature) for HMAC and `jsonpath-rust` (already a hard dep) for capture. `hex` would be the only addition if hex-encoding is needed (base64 is present).

**`crates/core/Cargo.toml`:** `reqwest` (hard, line 112), `jsonpath-rust` (hard, 113), `async-trait` (111). `sha2`/`hmac`/`base64` in core are **optional**, gated behind `masking`/`encryption`/`transform-hash` features — not on by default. Core's `FaucetError` variants (`crates/core/src/error.rs`): `Auth(String)` (30), `Config(String)` (48), `Source(String)` (52), `State(String)` (79), `HttpStatus{status,..}` (18) with `is_retryable` treating ≥500/429 as retryable.

### `StateStore` (for persistence/dynamic-capture, #499 pattern)
`Arc&lt;dyn StateStore&gt;` with `async fn get(&amp;self,key)-&gt;Result&lt;Option&lt;Value&gt;&gt;`, `put(&amp;self,key,&amp;Value)`, `delete(&amp;self,key)` (seen in oauth2.rs tests lines 646–656). `FileStateStore` and `MemoryStateStore` are exported from `faucet_core`. The oauth2_refresh provider is the reference for durable capture/rotation the new flow provider can mirror for dynamic base-URL / captured-value persistence.

---

### Summary of extension points for #511
- Add a module `crates/auth/src/flow.rs` + a `"flow"` arm in `build_provider` (`lib.rs:74`).
- Implement `AuthProvider`: use `credential()` for a captured reusable credential and/or override `sign_request` (already object-safe, `auth.rs:133`) for the HMAC signer.
- Reuse: `jsonpath_rust::JsonPath` capture (`token_endpoint.rs:274` pattern), `ApplyAs`-style placement (`token_endpoint.rs:26` — extend to query/cookie/body), oauth1 HMAC (`oauth1.rs:136`), `Mutex`-single-flight + `invalidate` CAS (oauth2/token_endpoint), and `StateStore` persistence (oauth2 `parse_persist`/`persist`/`ensure_loaded`).
- Consumption is already wired: `registry.rs` injects via `with_auth_provider`; REST already calls `sign_request` then `credential`. For "reauth-on-status" beyond REST's inline-only 401 path (`stream.rs:625`), the shared-provider `invalidate` hook exists but is not yet called at that site.</result>
